 1012: <<<<<<< HEAD
 1013: /// Splits a comic title into the fragments offered as keyword choices, cutting
 1014: /// on commas and bracketed groups.
 1015: List<String> _splitText(String text) {
 1016:   var words = <String>[];
 1017:   var buffer = StringBuffer();
 1018:   var inBracket = false;
 1019:   String? prevBracket;
 1020:   for (var i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
 1021:     var c = text[i];
 1022:     if (c == '[' || c == '(') {
 1023:       if (inBracket) {
 1024:         buffer.write(c);
 1025:       } else {
 1026:         if (buffer.isNotEmpty) {
 1027:           words.add(buffer.toString().trim());
 1028:           buffer.clear();
 1029:         }
 1030:         inBracket = true;
 1031:         prevBracket = c;
 1032:       }
 1033:     } else if (c == ']' || c == ')') {
 1034:       if (prevBracket == '[' && c == ']' || prevBracket == '(' && c == ')') {
 1035:         if (buffer.isNotEmpty) {
 1036:           words.add(buffer.toString().trim());
 1037:           buffer.clear();
 1038:         }
 1039:         inBracket = false;
 1040:       } else {
 1041:         buffer.write(c);
 1042:       }
 1043:     } else if (c == ',') {
 1044:       if (inBracket) {
 1045:         buffer.write(c);
 1046:       } else {
 1047:         words.add(buffer.toString().trim());
 1048:         buffer.clear();
 1049:       }
 1050:     } else {
 1051:       buffer.write(c);
 1052:     }
 1053:   }
 1054:   if (buffer.isNotEmpty) {
 1055:     words.add(buffer.toString().trim());
 1056:   }
 1057:   words.removeWhere((element) => element == "");
 1058:   return words.toSet().toList();
 1059: }
 1060: 
 1061: /// Lets the user turn a comic's own words and tags into blocklist entries.
 1062: ///
 1063: /// Title fragments go to the keyword list (substring-matched against
 1064: /// title/subtitle/description) and tags go to the tag list, so a tag pick can
 1065: /// never accidentally hide comics whose *title* happens to contain that word.
 1066: /// Shared by the list long-press menu and the comic detail page, where the full
 1067: /// tag set is always available.
 1068: void showBlockDialog({
 1069:   required String title,
 1070:   String? subtitle,
 1071:   required List<String> tags,
 1072:   VoidCallback? onBlocked,
 1073: }) {

 1075:     ..._splitText(title),
 1076:     if (subtitle != null && subtitle.isNotEmpty) subtitle,

 1087:             List<String> items,
 1088:             List<String> picked, {
 1089:             bool translate = false,
 1090:           }) {
 1091:             return Column(
 1092:               crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 1093:               children: [
 1094:                 Text(title, style: ts.s12.bold),

 1097:                   runSpacing: 8,
 1098:                   spacing: 8,
 1099:                   children: [
 1100:                     for (var item in items)
 1101:                       OptionChip(
 1102:                         // Only tags have translations; a title fragment must be
 1103:                         // shown as-is. Picking a tag stores its value half (the
 1104:                         // part after the namespace), so the entry also matches
 1105:                         // comics that carry the tag without a namespace.

 1108:                                 .split(':')

 1116:                               picked.add(item);
 1117:                             }

 1342:   int _visibleRowCount(double maxHeight, bool hasRating) {
 1343:     if (maxHeight.isInfinite) {
 1344:       return hasRating ? 4 : 5;
 1345:     }
 1346:     final reservedHeight = (showTitle ? 24 : 0) + (hasRating ? 20 : 0);
 1347:     final count = ((maxHeight - reservedHeight) / 21).floor();
 1348:     return math.max(1, math.min(5, count));
 1349:   }

 1479:   String? _clean(String? value) {
 1480:     final result = value?.replaceAll("\n", " ").trim();
 1481:     return result == null ||
 1482:             result.isEmpty ||
 1483:             result == "Unknown" ||
 1484:             result.startsWith("Unknown:")
 1485:         ? null
 1486:         : result;
 1487:   }
 1488: 
 1489:   String? _tagText(List<_DescriptionTag> rawTags) {
 1490:     if (rawTags.isEmpty) {
 1491:       return null;
 1492:     }
 1493:     return rawTags.map((tag) => tag.label).join(" / ");
 1494:   }
 1495: 
 1496:   List<_DescriptionTag> _tagItems() {
 1497:     final rawTags = tags
 1498:         ?.map((e) => e.replaceAll("\n", " ").trim())
 1499:         .where(
 1500:           (e) =>
 1501:               e.removeAllBlank != "" &&
 1502:               !_isMetadataTag(e) &&
 1503:               _clean(e.split(':').last) != null,
 1504:         )
 1505:         .toList();
 1506:     if (rawTags == null || rawTags.isEmpty) {
 1507:       return const [];
 1508:     }
 1509:     final enableTranslate =
 1510:         App.locale.languageCode == 'zh' && this.enableTranslate;
 1511:     return rawTags.map((tag) {
 1512:       final index = tag.indexOf(':');
 1513:       final namespace = index == -1 ? null : tag.substring(0, index);
 1514:       final value = index == -1 ? tag : tag.substring(index + 1);
 1515:       return _DescriptionTag(
 1516:         namespace: namespace,
 1517:         value: value,
 1518:         label: enableTranslate
 1519:             ? TagsTranslation.translateTag(tag)
 1520:             : tag.split(':').last,
 1521:       );
 1522:     }).toList();
 1523:   }

 1526:     // Only structured author metadata identifies an author. A subtitle can be
 1527:     // a status, language or arbitrary description, and commas can be in names.
 1528:     return _tagItemsWithNamespace(_authorNamespaces);
 1529:   }

 1531:   String? _statusText() {
 1532:     return _tagsWithNamespace(_statusNamespaces).firstOrNull;
 1533:   }

 1546:   List<String> _tagsWithNamespace(Set<String> namespaces) {
 1547:     return _tagItemsWithNamespace(namespaces).map((e) => e.label).toList();
 1548:   }

 1550:   List<_DescriptionTag> _tagItemsWithNamespace(Set<String> namespaces) {
 1551:     return tags
 1552:             ?.where((e) => e.contains(':'))
 1553:             .map((e) {
 1554:               final index = e.indexOf(':');
 1555:               final namespace = _normalizeNamespace(e.substring(0, index));
 1556:               final rawValue = e.substring(index + 1);
 1557:               final value = _authorNamespaces.contains(namespace)
 1558:                   ? (rawValue.trim().isEmpty ? null : rawValue)
 1559:                   : _clean(rawValue);
 1560:               if (value == null || !namespaces.contains(namespace)) {
 1561:                 return null;
 1562:               }
 1563:               return _DescriptionTag(
 1564:                 namespace: e.substring(0, index),
 1565:                 value: value,
 1566:                 label: enableTranslate && App.locale.languageCode == 'zh'
 1567:                     ? TagsTranslation.translateTag(e)
 1568:                     : value,
 1569:               );
 1570:             })
 1571:             .whereType<_DescriptionTag>()
 1572:             .toList() ??
 1573:         const [];
 1574:   }
 1575: 
 1576:   bool _isMetadataTag(String tag) {
 1577:     if (!tag.contains(':')) {
 1578:       final value = _clean(tag);
 1579:       return value == null || _looksLikeDate(value) || _looksLikeStatus(value);
 1580:     }
 1581:     final index = tag.indexOf(':');
 1582:     final namespace = _normalizeNamespace(tag.substring(0, index));
 1583:     final value = _clean(tag.substring(index + 1));
 1584:     return _metadataNamespaces.contains(namespace) ||
 1585:         value == null ||
 1586:         _looksLikeDate(value) ||
 1587:         _looksLikeStatus(value);
 1588:   }

 1658:   String? _fallbackDescription(

 1703:       '休载',
 1704:     }.contains(normalized);
 1705:   }

 1707:   bool _isMetadataDescription(List<String> parts, String? source) {

 1712:     return source == null ||
 1713:         descriptionSource == null ||
 1714:         descriptionSource == source;

  723:   /// add history. if exists, update time.
  724:   ///
  725:   /// This function would be called when user start reading.
  726:   /// 記錄「上上次閱讀」位置：寫入新進度前，把舊記錄存起來。
  727:   /// 存在 appdata.implicitData['prevReadPos']，key = 'typeValue:id'。
  728:   void _recordPreviousPosition(History newItem) {
  729:     try {
  730:       if (!isInitialized || _isCorrupted) return;
  731:       final key = '${newItem.type.value}:${newItem.id}';
  732:       // Match getAll's visibility semantics, but use the composite primary
  733:       // key instead of materializing/sorting the entire history table. Do not
  734:       // use find(): its cached History may already contain the new position.
  735:       final old = _db.select(
  736:         '''select ep, page, chapter_group, time from history
  737:            where id == ? and type == ? and ifnull(hidden, 0) == 0
  738:            limit 1;''',
  739:         [newItem.id, newItem.type.value],
  740:       );
  741:       if (old.isEmpty) return;
  742:       final prev = old.first;
  743:       // 同一話內翻頁不覆蓋「上上次」——只有【換話】才算新的一次閱讀
  744:       //（否則上上次會變成「上一頁」，沒有意義）
  745:       if (prev['ep'] == newItem.ep) return;
  746:       final store = appdata.implicitData['prevReadPos'];
  747:       final map = store is Map ? Map<String, dynamic>.from(store) : <String, dynamic>{};
  748:       // 舊格式（單筆 Map）兼容 → 統一成 List（最近在前，最多 3 筆）
  749:       final existing = map[key];
  750:       final list = existing is List
  751:           ? List<dynamic>.from(existing)
  752:           : (existing is Map ? [Map<String, dynamic>.from(existing)] : <dynamic>[]);
  753:       final entry = {
  754:         'ep': prev['ep'],
  755:         'page': prev['page'],
  756:         'group': prev['chapter_group'],
  757:         'time': DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(
  758:           prev['time'] as int,
  759:         ).toIso8601String(),
  760:       };
  761:       // 去重：同話同頁不重複記
  762:       final dup = list.any((e) =>
  763:           e is Map && e['ep'] == entry['ep'] && e['page'] == entry['page']);
  764:       if (!dup) list.insert(0, entry);
  765:       if (list.length > 3) list.removeRange(3, list.length);
  766:       map[key] = list;
  767:       appdata.implicitData['prevReadPos'] = map;
  768:       appdata.writeImplicitData();
  769:     } catch (_) {
  770:       // 純附加功能，失敗不影響主流程
  771:     }
  772:   }

  694:   /// Determines the language from the recognized script; falls back to the
  695:   /// engine's own language when the text is ambiguous.
  696:   String _detectLanguage(String text, String engineLang) {
  697:     var kana = 0, hangul = 0, han = 0, latin = 0;
  698:     for (var r in text.runes) {
  699:       if ((r >= 0x3040 && r <= 0x30FF) || (r >= 0x31F0 && r <= 0x31FF)) {
  700:         kana++;
  701:       } else if ((r >= 0xAC00 && r <= 0xD7AF) || (r >= 0x1100 && r <= 0x11FF)) {
  702:         hangul++;
  703:       } else if ((r >= 0x4E00 && r <= 0x9FFF) || (r >= 0x3400 && r <= 0x4DBF)) {
  704:         han++;
  705:       } else if ((r >= 0x41 && r <= 0x5A) || (r >= 0x61 && r <= 0x7A)) {
  706:         latin++;
  707:       }
  708:     }
  709:     if (kana > 0) return 'ja';
  710:     if (hangul > 0) return 'ko';
  711:     if (han > 0) {
  712:       return engineLang == 'ja' ? 'ja' :
  713:           engineLang == 'zh-TW' ? 'zh-TW' : 'zh';
  714:     }
  715:     if (latin > 0) return 'en';
  716:     return engineLang;
  717:   }
  718:

   31:   void replace(Object? value) {
   32:     final next = HashSet<JSInvokable>.identity();
   33:     final visited = HashSet<Object>.identity();
   34:     void visit(Object? node) {
   35:       if (node == null || !visited.add(node)) return;
   36:       if (node is JSInvokable) {
   37:         next.add(node);
   38:       } else if (node is Map) {
   39:         for (final entry in node.entries) {
   40:           visit(entry.key);
   41:           visit(entry.value);
   42:         }
   43:       } else if (node is List) {
   44:         for (final item in node) {
   45:           visit(item);
   46:         }
   47:       }
   48:     }
   49:     visit(value);
   50:     final previous = _handles;
   51:     _handles = next;

   53:       // Config exports are exclusively owned by this task. The bridge may
   54:       // add nested Promise reference counts, so end ownership explicitly.
   55:       if (!next.contains(handle)) handle.destroy();
   56:     }
   57:   }
   58: 
   59:   void retain(Object? value) => replace([value, ..._handles]);

  629:   @override
  630:   void removeListener(VoidCallback listener) {
  631:     super.removeListener(listener);
  632:     _borrowers.remove(listener);
  633:     // The manager prunes after the synchronous detach/reattach transaction.
  634:     onBorrowersChanged();
  635:     _finishDisposal();
  636:   }
  637: 
  638:   // AnimatedBuilder owns the actual borrow: it can detach and reattach its
  639:   // merged Listenable during one synchronous update, even after the reader
  640:   // retires this generation. Never dispose between those two operations.
  641:   void _finishDisposal() {
  642:     // hasListeners may still include a removed listener while ChangeNotifier
  643:     // is notifying. Check it only after that notification has unwound.
  644:     if (!_retired || _released || _releaseScheduled) return;
  645:     _releaseScheduled = true;
  646:     scheduleMicrotask(() {
  647:       _releaseScheduled = false;
  648:       if (_released || hasListeners) return;
  649:       _released = true;
  650:       super.dispose();
  651:     });
  652:   }
  653: 
  654:   // Ownership ends now; actual ChangeNotifier disposal waits for borrowers.
  655:   // Do not override dispose with a deferred super call (@mustCallSuper).
  656:   void retire() {
  657:     if (_retired) return;
  658:     _retired = true;
  659:     // Cancel generations now, not when the last footer eventually detaches.
  660:     invalidate();
  661:     final listener = _ownerListener;
  662:     _ownerListener = null;
  663:     if (listener != null) super.removeListener(listener);
  664:     _finishDisposal();
  665:   }

  673: class ReaderChapterCommentsFeeds {
  674:   ReaderChapterCommentsFeeds({this.onChanged}) {
  675:     ComicSource.settingsChanges.addListener(_settingsChanged);
  676:   }
  677: 
  678:   /// Recent current/prefetch lookups form a conservative reader window. Real
  679:   /// consumers are protected in addition to this many unborrowed chapters.
  680:   static const int retainedIdleChapters = 8;
  681:   final Map<String, (ComicSource, String, String)> _requests = {};
  682:   final Map<String, String> _revisions = {};
  683:   final Map<String, _ProviderChapterFeed> _feeds = {};
  684:   // A settings replacement may not acquire its AnimatedBuilder until the next
  685:   // frame. Keep its key protected while any previous generation is borrowed.
  686:   final Map<String, List<_ProviderChapterFeed>> _retiringBorrowers = {};
  687:   bool _disposed = false;
  688:   bool _pruneScheduled = false;
  689:   bool _reloading = false;
  690:   final void Function(String epId, String revision, CommentsController feed)? onChanged;
  691: 
  692:   bool _isBorrowed(String key) => _feeds[key]?.hasBorrowers == true ||
  693:       (_retiringBorrowers[key]?.any((feed) => feed.hasBorrowers) ?? false);
  694: 
  695:   void _schedulePrune() {
  696:     if (_disposed || _pruneScheduled) return;
  697:     _pruneScheduled = true;
  698:     scheduleMicrotask(() {
  699:       _pruneScheduled = false;
  700:       if (!_disposed) _prune();
  701:     });
  702:   }
  703: 
  704:   void _prune() {
  705:     if (_reloading) return;
  706:     for (final key in _retiringBorrowers.keys.toList()) {
  707:       _retiringBorrowers[key]!.removeWhere((feed) => !feed.hasBorrowers);
  708:       if (_retiringBorrowers[key]!.isEmpty) _retiringBorrowers.remove(key);
  709:     }
  710:     final idle = _requests.keys.where((key) => !_isBorrowed(key)).toList();
  711:     for (final key in idle.take(idle.length > retainedIdleChapters
  712:         ? idle.length - retainedIdleChapters : 0)) {
  713:       _requests.remove(key);
  714:       _revisions.remove(key);
  715:       _feeds.remove(key)?.retire();
  716:     }
  717:   }
  718: 
  719:   void _settingsChanged() {
  720:     if (_disposed) return;
  721:     _prune();
  722:     final changed = _requests.entries.where((entry) =>
  723:       _revisions[entry.key] != entry.value.$1.commentScopeRevision).toList();
  724:     _reloading = true;
  725:     try {
  726:       for (final entry in changed) {
  727:         final old = _feeds.remove(entry.key);
  728:         if (old != null) {
  729:           if (old.hasBorrowers) {
  730:             (_retiringBorrowers[entry.key] ??= []).add(old);
  731:           }
  732:           // Cancel immediately; actual disposal still waits for borrowers.
  733:           old.retire();
  734: =======

 1057:   void _settingsChanged() {
 1058:     if (_scopeRevision == widget.source.commentScopeRevision) return;
 1059:     // ReaderChapterCommentsFeeds owns invalidation/replacement for a borrowed
 1060:     // feed. The reader's next build passes that replacement; do not also issue

 1066:       if (!mounted) return;
 1067:       setState(() {
 1068:         if (_ownsFeed) feed.dispose();
 1069:         _createFeed();
 1070:       });
 1071:     });

 1075:   void _createFeed() {

 1112:   // Natural chapter content; old persisted height multipliers are inert.
 1113:   static const previewLimit = 4;
 1114:   @override
 1115:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 1116:     final hasSelector = widget.source.commentProviderOptions.isNotEmpty;
 1117:     if (!widget.source.hasChapterComments && !hasSelector) return const SizedBox.shrink();
 1118:     return AnimatedBuilder(animation: Listenable.merge([feed, appdata.settings]),
 1119:       builder: (context, _) {
 1120:         if ((feed.unavailable || feed.confirmedEmpty) && !hasSelector) return const SizedBox.shrink();
 1121:         final sorts = widget.source.availableCommentSorts(chapter: true, provider: feed.effectiveProvider);
 1122:         final sortSelector = CommentSortSelector(sorts: sorts, value: feed.sort,
 1123:           onSelected: (sort) async {
 1124:             try { await widget.source.setPreferredCommentSort(sort, chapter: true); }
 1125:             catch (_) { if (mounted) context.showMessage(message: '评论排序保存失败'); }
 1126:           });
 1127:         if (widget.horizontal) {
 1128:           // The Material host (and the DefaultTextStyle it installs) sits above
 1129:           // the measurement builder, so chunking sees exactly the text style
 1130:           // the painted Text widgets below will use.
 1131:           return Material(color: context.colorScheme.surface,
 1132:             child: LayoutBuilder(builder: (context, constraints) {
 1133:           final width = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width;
 1134:           // Each semantic section occupies a chapter-scroll panel. No nested
 1135:           // scrollable: at large text/short heights the reply action gets its
 1136:           // own reachable panel instead of overflowing below the viewport.
 1137:           Widget panel(List<Widget> children) => SizedBox(width: width,
 1138:             child: Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
 1139:               child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
 1140:                 children: children)));
 1141:           Widget preview(String text, {TextStyle? style, int maxLines = 2}) => Expanded(
 1142:             child: LayoutBuilder(builder: (context, constraints) {
 1143:               final base = style?.fontSize ?? 14;
 1144:               final lineHeight = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(base) * 1.5;
 1145:               final lines = (constraints.maxHeight / lineHeight).floor();
 1146:               if (lines < 1) return const SizedBox.shrink();
 1147:               return Text(text, maxLines: lines.clamp(1, maxLines), overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
 1148:                 style: (style ?? const TextStyle()).copyWith(height: 1.5));
 1149:             }));
 1150:           // Unlike the old ellipsized body, every measured line gets a panel.
 1151:           // This keeps a finite horizontal chapter viewport free of inner scroll.
 1152:           // Chunk boundaries come from measuring the exact substring that will
 1153:           // be painted. Extrapolating from caret/line indices can place one
 1154:           // extra rendered line in a panel at large text scales (a wrapped line
 1155:           // start maps back to the previous line's baseline), which overflows
 1156:           // the finite viewport by a line and clips its last line.
 1157:           final panelWidth = math.max(1.0,
 1158:             (constraints.hasBoundedWidth ? constraints.maxWidth : width) - 32);
 1159:           final available = math.max(1.0, constraints.maxHeight - 32);
 1160:           double paintedHeight(String value, TextStyle style) {
 1161:             final painter = TextPainter(text: TextSpan(text: value, style: style),
 1162:               textDirection: Directionality.of(context),
 1163:               textScaler: MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context))..layout(maxWidth: panelWidth);
 1164:             final height = painter.height;
 1165:             painter.dispose();
 1166:             return height;
 1167:           }
 1168:           List<Widget> textPanels(String text, {double? fontSize}) {
 1169:             if (text.isEmpty) return const [];
 1170:             // Text merges the ambient DefaultTextStyle (letter spacing, font
 1171:             // family, …) into the explicit style, so measure that exact merged
 1172:             // style: measuring the bare style can wrap one glyph earlier than
 1173:             // the painted text and bring back the overflowing line.
 1174:             final style = DefaultTextStyle.of(context).style.merge(
 1175:               TextStyle(fontSize: fontSize ?? commentsFontSize, height: 1.5));
 1176:             final result = <Widget>[];
 1177:             var start = 0;
 1178:             while (start < text.length) {
 1179:               // Largest prefix of the remaining text whose painted height fits
 1180:               // the panel. Height grows monotonically with the prefix, so a
 1181:               // binary search over character offsets is exact and cheap.
 1182:               var low = start + 1, high = text.length, end = start;
 1183:               while (low <= high) {
 1184:                 final mid = (low + high) ~/ 2;
 1185:                 var candidate = mid;
 1186:                 // Never cut a surrogate pair in half.
 1187:                 if (candidate < text.length &&
 1188:                     (text.codeUnitAt(candidate) & 0xFC00) == 0xDC00) {
 1189:                   candidate--;
 1190:                 }
 1191:                 if (candidate <= start) { low = mid + 1; continue; }
 1192:                 if (paintedHeight(text.substring(start, candidate), style) <= available) {
 1193:                   end = candidate;
 1194:                   low = mid + 1;
 1195:                 } else {
 1196:                   high = mid - 1;
 1197:                 }
 1198:               }
 1199:               if (end <= start) {
 1200:                 // Degenerate viewport: no full line fits. Keep the text
 1201:                 // reachable as the single ellipsized line the panel can paint.
 1202:                 result.add(panel([Text(text.substring(start), style: style,
 1203:                   maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis)]));
 1204:                 break;
 1205:               }
 1206:               result.add(panel([Text(text.substring(start, end), style: style)]));
 1207:               start = end;
 1208:             }
 1209:             return result;
 1210:           }
 1211:           return Row(
 1212:             textDirection:widget.reverse ? TextDirection.rtl : TextDirection.ltr,
 1213:             key:const ValueKey('chapter-comments-region'),
 1214:             crossAxisAlignment:CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,children:[
 1215:             panel([
 1216:               Row(children: [
 1217:                 Expanded(child: Text('Chapter Comments'.tl, style: ts.s18)),
 1218:                 CommentProviderSelector(source: widget.source),
 1219:               ]),
 1220:               preview(widget.chapterTitle),
 1221:               TextButton(onPressed:_openAll,child:Text('View all comments'.tl)),
 1222:             ]),
 1223:             panel([
 1224:               if (sorts.length > 1) sortSelector,
 1225:               IconButton(
 1226:                 key: const ValueKey('refresh-chapter-comments'),
 1227:                 tooltip: '刷新评论',
 1228:                 icon: feed.loading
 1229:                     ? const SizedBox(width: 18, height: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
 1230:                     : const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
 1231:                 onPressed: feed.loading ? null : feed.refresh,
 1232:                 visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
 1233:               ),
 1234:               CommentContextStats(context: feed.commentContext),
 1235:             ]),
 1236:             if(feed.loading) panel([const Center(child:CircularProgressIndicator())]),
 1237:             if(feed.error!=null) panel([
 1238:               preview(feed.error!),
 1239:               TextButton(onPressed:feed.refresh,child:const Text('重试')),
 1240:             ]),
 1241:             if(!feed.loading && feed.error==null)
 1242:               for(final comment in feed.comments.take(previewLimit)) ...[
 1243:                 panel([CommentAvatar(url:comment.avatar,sourceKey:widget.source.key),
 1244:                   preview(comment.userName)]),
 1245:                 panel([CommentMetadata(comment: comment),
 1246:                   if (comment.time != null) preview(comment.time!, style: ts.s12)]),
 1247:                 panel([CommentReadOnlyStats(comment:comment,onReply:
 1248:                   widget.source.canReadReplies(comment,chapter:true,provider:feed.effectiveProvider) ? ()=>_openReply(comment) : null)]),
 1249:                 ...textPanels(comment.content),
 1250:                 for (final reply in comment.effectiveReplyPreviews) ...[

  357:   Timer? _precacheTimer;
  358: 
  359:   /// Defers neighbor precaching until the page has settled. [cache] used to be
  360:   /// invoked from the page builder — i.e. exactly while the page-turn
  361:   /// animation was running — so full-resolution decodes and texture uploads
  362:   /// competed with the animation frames and read as dropped frames (#107).
  363:   /// The delay also coalesces rapid flips into a single cache pass.
  364:   void _schedulePrecache(int page) {
  365:     _precacheTimer?.cancel();
  366:     _precacheTimer = Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 200), () {
  367:       if (!mounted || reader.page != page || isChapterCommentsPage(page)) {
  368:         return;
  369:       }
  370:       cache(page);
  371:     });
  372:   }
  373: 
  374:   void _increaseFingers() {
  375:     fingers++;
  376:   }
  377: 
  378:   void _decreaseFingers() {
  379:     if (fingers > 0) {
  380:       fingers--;
  381:     } else {
  382:       fingers = 0;
  383:     }
  384:   }
  385: 
  386:   /// Get the range of images for the given page. [page] is 1-based.
  387:   (int start, int end) getPageImagesRange(int page) {
  388:     var imagesPerPage = reader.imagesPerPage;
  389:     if (reader.showSingleImageOnFirstPage()) {
  390:       if (page == 1) {
  391:         return (0, 1);
  392:       } else {
  393:         int startIndex = (page - 2) * imagesPerPage + 1;
  394:         int endIndex = math.min(
  395:           startIndex + imagesPerPage,
  396:           reader.images!.length,
  397:         );
  398:         return (startIndex, endIndex);
  399:       }
  400:     } else {
  401:       int startIndex = (page - 1) * imagesPerPage;
  402:       int endIndex = math.min(
  403:         startIndex + imagesPerPage,
  404:         reader.images!.length,
  405:       );
  406:       return (startIndex, endIndex);
  407:     }
  408:   }
  409: 
  410:   /// Get the image indices for current page. Returns null if no images.
  411:   /// Returns a single index if only one image, or a range if multiple images.
  412:   (int, int)? getCurrentPageImageRange() {
  413:     if (reader.images == null || reader.images!.isEmpty) {
  414:       return null;
  415:     }
  416:     // 评论页没有图片，按页码算出的起始索引会越过图片列表，提前排除以免越界
  417:     if (reader.isOnChapterCommentsPage) {
  418:       return null;
  419:     }
  420:     var (startIndex, endIndex) = getPageImagesRange(reader.page);
  421:     if (startIndex >= reader.images!.length) {
  422:       return null;
  423:     }
  424:     return (startIndex, endIndex);
  425:   }
  426: 
  427:   void cache(int startPage) {
  428:     // 向前"解码"预取的页数（不含仅下载）。只有解码进内存的页在滑入视口时
  429:     // 才是现成纹理、不会临场解码掉帧；但 gallery 图为支持 pinch-zoom 保留全
  430:     // 分辨率，解码占内存较大，故解码窗口远小于下载窗口 [preCacheCount] 并封顶
  431:     // 到 2。向后保留 1 页（返回上一页时同样流畅）。超出解码窗口的页仍只下载，
  432:     // 由 [_cachePage] 走 _preDownloadImage。
  433:     final decodeAhead = preCacheCount.clamp(1, 2);
  434:     for (int i = startPage - 1; i <= startPage + preCacheCount; i++) {
  435:       if (i == startPage ||
  436:           i <= 0 ||
  437:           i > totalPages ||
  438:           isChapterCommentsPage(i)) {
  439:         continue;
  440:       }
  441:       final shouldPreCache =
  442:           i == startPage - 1 || (i > startPage && i <= startPage + decodeAhead);
  443:       _cachePage(i, shouldPreCache);
  444:     }
  445:   }
  446: 
  447:   void _cachePage(int page, bool shouldPreCache) {
  448:     if (isChapterCommentsPage(page)) return;
  449:     var (startIndex, endIndex) = getPageImagesRange(page);
  450:     for (int i = startIndex; i < endIndex; i++) {
  451:       shouldPreCache
  452:           ? _precacheImage(i + 1, context)
  453:           : _preDownloadImage(i + 1, context);
  454:     }
  455:   }
  456: 
  457:   Widget _buildChapterCommentsPage() {
  458:     var source = ComicSource.find(reader.type.sourceKey);
  459:     var chapters = reader.widget.chapters;
  460:     if (source == null || chapters == null) return const SizedBox();
  461:     var chapterIndex = reader.chapter - 1;
  462:     WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _resolveFooterEnd());
  463:     return NotificationListener<ScrollMetricsNotification>(
  464:       onNotification: (_) {
  465:         WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _resolveFooterEnd());

  472:         source: source,

  474:         chapterTitle: chapters.titles.elementAt(chapterIndex),
  475:         sharedFeed: reader.chapterCommentsFeed(source, chapters.ids.elementAt(chapterIndex)),
  476:       ),
  477:     ));

  625: 
  626:   Widget buildPageImages(List<String> images, int startIndex) {
  627:     Axis axis = (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryTopToBottom)
  628:         ? Axis.vertical
  629:         : Axis.horizontal;
  630: 
  631:     bool reverse = reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryRightToLeft;
  632:     if (reverse) {
  633:       images = images.reversed.toList();
  634:     }
  635: 
  636:     List<Widget> imageWidgets;
  637: 
  638:     if (images.length == 2) {
  639:       imageWidgets = [
  640:         Expanded(
  641:           child: ComicImage(
  642:             width: double.infinity,
  643:             height: double.infinity,
  644:             image: _createImageProviderFromKey(
  645:               images[0],
  646:               context,
  647:               readerImagePageOrdinal(startIndex, 2, 0, reversed: reverse),
  648:             ),
  649:             fit: BoxFit.contain,
  650:             alignment: axis == Axis.vertical
  651:                 ? Alignment.bottomCenter
  652:                 : Alignment.centerRight,
  653:             onInit: (state) => imageStates.add(state),
  654:             onDispose: (state) => imageStates.remove(state),
  655:           ),
  656:         ),
  657:         Expanded(
  658:           child: ComicImage(
  659:             width: double.infinity,
  660:             height: double.infinity,
  661:             image: _createImageProviderFromKey(
  662:               images[1],
  663:               context,
  664:               readerImagePageOrdinal(startIndex, 2, 1, reversed: reverse),
  665:             ),
  666:             fit: BoxFit.contain,
  667:             alignment: axis == Axis.vertical
  668:                 ? Alignment.topCenter
  669:                 : Alignment.centerLeft,
  670:             onInit: (state) => imageStates.add(state),
  671:             onDispose: (state) => imageStates.remove(state),
  672:           ),
  673:         ),
  674:       ];
  675:     } else {
  676:       imageWidgets = images.asMap().entries.map((entry) {
  677:         final imageKey = entry.value;
  678:         final ordinal = readerImagePageOrdinal(startIndex, images.length, entry.key,
  679:             reversed: reverse);
  680:         ImageProvider imageProvider = _createImageProviderFromKey(
  681:           imageKey,
  682:           context,
  683:           ordinal,
  684:         );
  685:         return Expanded(
  686:           child: ComicImage(
  687:             image: imageProvider,
  688:             fit: BoxFit.contain,
  689:             onInit: (state) => imageStates.add(state),
  690:             onDispose: (state) => imageStates.remove(state),
  691:           ),
  692:         );
  693:       }).toList();
  694:     }
  695: 
  696:     return axis == Axis.vertical
  697:         ? Column(children: imageWidgets)
  698:         : Row(children: imageWidgets);
  699:   }
  700: 
  701:   @override
  702:   Future<void> animateToPage(int page, {Duration? duration}) {
  703:     if ((page - controller.page!.round()).abs() > 1) {
  704:       controller.jumpToPage(page > controller.page! ? page - 1 : page + 1);
  705:     }
  706:     return controller.animateToPage(
  707:       page,
  708:       duration: duration ?? const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
  709:       curve: Curves.ease,
  710:     );
  711:   }
  712: 
  713:   // 畫廊模式不支持連續自動滾動（本質是一頁頁），走定時翻頁
  714:   @override
  715:   bool get supportsAutoScroll => false;
  716: 
  717:   @override
  718:   bool get autoScrolling => false;
  719: 
  720:   @override
  721:   void startAutoScroll(double pixelsPerSecond) {}
  722: 
  723:   @override
  724:   void onCommentsChanged() {
  725:     if (!mounted) return;
  726:     setState(() {});
  727:     WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
  728:       if (!mounted) return;
  729:       if (controller.hasClients && (controller.page ?? 0) > totalPages) {
  730:         controller.jumpToPage(totalPages);
  731:       }
  732:       _resolveFooterEnd();
  733:     });
  734:   }
  735: 
  736:   @override
  737:   void stopAutoScroll() {}
  738: 
  739:   @override
  740:   void toPage(int page) {
  741:     controller.jumpToPage(page);
  742:   }
  743: 
  744:   @override
  745:   bool turnPage(bool forward) {
  746:     if (!reader.isOnChapterCommentsPage || !footerScrollController.hasClients) return false;
  747:     final pos = footerScrollController.position;
  748:     if (forward && pos.extentAfter > 1 || !forward && pos.extentBefore > 1) {
  749:       final target = (pos.pixels + (forward ? 1 : -1) * pos.viewportDimension)
  750:           .clamp(pos.minScrollExtent,pos.maxScrollExtent);
  751:       unawaited(footerScrollController.animateTo(target,
  752:         duration:const Duration(milliseconds:200),curve:Curves.ease));
  753:       return true;
  754:     }
  755:     // The footer is already fully on screen. Do not consume the turn while it
  756:     // is pending/errored: the gesture caller falls back to chapter navigation,
  757:     // so an unreachable comment source cannot pin the reader here forever.
  758:     return false;
  759:   }
  760: 
  761:   @override
  762:   bool jumpToChapter(int chapter, {bool toLastPage = false}) => false; // gallery keyed by chapter; uses the default remount path
  763: 
  764:   @override
  765:   bool get isImageZoomed {
  766:     // 每页各自一个 PhotoViewController，取当前页的判断缩放；评论页等无控制器视为未缩放。
  767:     final controller = photoViewControllers[reader.page];
  768:     final scale = controller?.scale;
  769:     if (scale == null) return false;
  770:     // 与本页的初始缩放（fit/铺满屏幕时为 covered，非 1.0）比较，铺满屏幕模式下
  771:     // 静止态就大于 1 也不误判为放大。
  772:     final initial = controller!.getInitialScale?.call() ?? 1.0;
  773:     return scale > initial * 1.01;
  774:   }
  775: 
  776:   @override
  777:   void handleDoubleTap(Offset location) {
  778:     if (reader.effectiveSetting('quickCollectImage') == 'DoubleTap') {
  779:       context.readerScaffold.addImageFavorite();
  780:       return;
  781:     }
  782:     // 评论页等非图片页没有对应的 PhotoViewController，置空保护避免空指针
  783:     var controller = photoViewControllers[reader.page];
  784:     controller?.onDoubleClick?.call();
  785:   }
  786: 
  787:   @override
  788:   void handleLongPressDown(Offset location) {
  789:     if (!reader.effectiveSetting('enableLongPressToZoom') || fingers != 1) {
  790:       return;
  791:     }
  792:     var photoViewController = photoViewControllers[reader.page];
  793:     if (photoViewController == null) return;
  794:     double target = photoViewController.getInitialScale!.call()! * 1.75;
  795:     var size = reader.size;
  796:     Offset zoomPosition;
  797:     if (reader.effectiveSetting('longPressZoomPosition') != 'center') {
  798:       zoomPosition = Offset(
  799:         size.width / 2 - location.dx,
  800:         size.height / 2 - location.dy,
  801:       );
  802:     } else {
  803:       zoomPosition = Offset(0, 0);
  804:     }
  805:     photoViewController.animateScale?.call(target, zoomPosition);
  806:     isLongPressing = true;
  807:   }
  808: 
  809:   @override
  810:   void handleLongPressUp(Offset location) {
  811:     if (!reader.effectiveSetting('enableLongPressToZoom') || !isLongPressing) {
  812:       return;
  813:     }
  814:     var photoViewController = photoViewControllers[reader.page];
  815:     if (photoViewController == null) {
  816:       isLongPressing = false;
  817:       return;
  818:     }
  819:     double target = photoViewController.getInitialScale!.call()!;
  820:     photoViewController.animateScale?.call(target);
  821:     isLongPressing = false;
  822:   }
  823: 
  824:   Timer? keyRepeatTimer;
  825: 
  826:   @override
  827:   void handleKeyEvent(KeyEvent event) {
  828:     bool? forward;
  829:     if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryLeftToRight &&
  830:         event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowRight) {
  831:       forward = true;
  832:     } else if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryRightToLeft &&
  833:         event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowLeft) {
  834:       forward = true;
  835:     } else if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryTopToBottom &&
  836:         event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowDown) {
  837:       forward = true;
  838:     } else if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryTopToBottom &&
  839:         event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowUp) {
  840:       forward = false;
  841:     } else if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryLeftToRight &&
  842:         event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowLeft) {
  843:       forward = false;
  844:     } else if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.galleryRightToLeft &&
  845:         event.logicalKey == LogicalKeyboardKey.arrowRight) {
  846:       forward = false;
  847:     }
  848:     if (event is KeyDownEvent) {
  849:       if (keyRepeatTimer != null) {
  850:         keyRepeatTimer!.cancel();
  851:         keyRepeatTimer = null;
  852:       }
  853:       if (forward == true) {
  854:         reader.toNextPage();
  855:       } else if (forward == false) {
  856:         reader.toPrevPage();
  857:       }
  858:     }
  859:     if (event is KeyRepeatEvent && keyRepeatTimer == null) {
  860:       keyRepeatTimer = Timer.periodic(
  861:         reader.enablePageAnimation(reader.cid, reader.type)
  862:             ? const Duration(milliseconds: 200)
  863:             : const Duration(milliseconds: 50),
  864:         (timer) {
  865:           if (!mounted) {
  866:             timer.cancel();
  867:             return;
  868:           } else if (forward == true) {
  869:             reader.toNextPage();
  870:           } else if (forward == false) {
  871:             reader.toPrevPage();
  872:           }
  873:         },
  874:       );
  875:     }
  876:     if (event is KeyUpEvent && keyRepeatTimer != null) {
  877:       keyRepeatTimer!.cancel();
  878:       keyRepeatTimer = null;
  879:     }
  880:   }
  881: 
  882:   @override
  883:   bool handleOnTap(Offset location) {
  884:     return false;
  885:   }
  886: 
  887:   @override
  888:   Future<Uint8List?> getImageByOffset(Offset offset) async {
  889:     ReaderImageProvider? provider;
  890:     for (var imageState in imageStates) {
  891:       if ((imageState as _ComicImageState).containsPoint(offset)) {
  892:         provider = imageState.widget.image as ReaderImageProvider;
  893:       }
  894:     }
  895:     if (provider == null) return null;
  896:     if (provider.imageKey.startsWith("file://")) {
  897:       return await File(provider.imageKey.substring(7)).readAsBytes();
  898:     } else {
  899:       final cache = await CacheManager().findCache(
  900:         "${provider.imageKey}@${provider.sourceKey}@${provider.cid}@${provider.eid}",
  901:       );
  902:       return cache?.readAsBytes();
  903:     }
  904:   }
  905: 
  906:   @override
  907:   String? getImageKeyByOffset(Offset offset) {
  908:     var range = getCurrentPageImageRange();
  909:     if (range == null) return null;
  910: 
  911:     var (startIndex, endIndex) = range;
  912:     int actualImageCount = endIndex - startIndex;
  913: 
  914:     if (actualImageCount == 1) {
  915:       return reader.images![startIndex];
  916:     }
  917: 
  918:     for (var imageState in imageStates) {
  919:       if ((imageState as _ComicImageState).containsPoint(offset)) {
  920:         var imageKey =
  921:             (imageState.widget.image as ReaderImageProvider).imageKey;
  922:         int index = reader.images!.indexOf(imageKey);
  923:         if (index >= startIndex && index < endIndex) {
  924:           return imageKey;
  925:         }
  926:       }
  927:     }
  928: 
  929:     return reader.images![startIndex];
  930:   }
  931: }
  932: 
  933: const Set<PointerDeviceKind> _kTouchLikeDeviceTypes = <PointerDeviceKind>{
  934:   PointerDeviceKind.touch,
  935:   PointerDeviceKind.mouse,
  936:   PointerDeviceKind.stylus,
  937:   PointerDeviceKind.invertedStylus,
  938:   PointerDeviceKind.unknown,
  939: };
  940: 
  941: const double _kChangeChapterOffset = 160;
  942: 
  943: /// 上滑／下滑切換下一章所需距離（像素）。
  944: /// 可在「閱讀設定」調整，值越小 → 不必滑那麼多就能切到下一章。
  945: double _globalChangeChapterOffset() {
  946:   final v = appdata.settings['changeChapterOffset'];
  947:   return (v is num && v > 0) ? v.toDouble() : _kChangeChapterOffset;
  948: }

 1029: /// Tracks the image's layout shell rather than ComicImage's async image state.
 1030: /// Deactivated global-key children must not participate until reactivated.
 1031: class _ContinuousImageGeometry extends StatefulWidget {
 1032:   const _ContinuousImageGeometry({required super.key, required this.owner,
 1033:     required this.entry, required this.child});
 1034:   final _ContinuousModeState owner;
 1035:   final _ContinuousReaderEntry entry;
 1036:   final Widget child;
 1037: 
 1038:   @override
 1039:   State<_ContinuousImageGeometry> createState() => _ContinuousImageGeometryState();
 1040: }
 1041: 
 1042: class _ContinuousImageGeometryState extends State<_ContinuousImageGeometry> {
 1043:   @override
 1044:   void initState() {
 1045:     super.initState();
 1046:     widget.owner._registerGeometry(this);
 1047:   }
 1048: 
 1049:   @override
 1050:   void didUpdateWidget(covariant _ContinuousImageGeometry oldWidget) {
 1051:     super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
 1052:     // Entry objects are rebuilt on chapter loads; keep the live value/order.
 1053:     oldWidget.owner._unregisterGeometry(this);
 1054:     widget.owner._registerGeometry(this);
 1055:   }
 1056: 
 1057:   @override
 1058:   void deactivate() {
 1059:     widget.owner._unregisterGeometry(this);
 1060:     super.deactivate();
 1061:   }
 1062: 
 1063:   @override
 1064:   void activate() {
 1065:     super.activate();
 1066:     widget.owner._registerGeometry(this);
 1067:   }
 1068: 
 1069:   @override
 1070:   void dispose() {
 1071:     widget.owner._unregisterGeometry(this);
 1072:     final owner = widget.owner;
 1073:     final id = '${widget.entry.chapter}:${widget.entry.page}';
 1074:     final key = widget.key as GlobalKey;
 1075:     // A same-frame repivot may reuse the key. Only retire it after the entire
 1076:     // frame if no replacement has acquired it; never remove a live target.
 1077:     WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
 1078:       if (key.currentContext == null && identical(owner._itemKeys[id], key)) {
 1079:         owner._itemKeys.remove(id);
 1080:       }
 1081:     });
 1082:     super.dispose();
 1083:   }
 1084: 
 1085:   @override
 1086:   Widget build(BuildContext context) => widget.child;
 1087: }
 1088: 
 1089: class _ContinuousModeState extends State<_ContinuousMode>
 1090:     with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin
 1091:     implements _ImageViewController {
 1092:   late _ReaderState reader;
 1093: 
 1094:   /// The reader's scroll controller. Owned directly now that the list is a
 1095:   /// plain [CustomScrollView] (the previous [ScrollablePositionedList] supplied
 1096:   /// one through a callback).
 1097:   final ScrollController _scrollController = ScrollController();
 1098: 
 1099:   ScrollController get scrollController => _scrollController;
 1100: 
 1101:   var photoViewController = PhotoViewController();
 1102: 
 1103:   var isCTRLPressed = false;
 1104:   static var _isMouseScrolling = false;
 1105:   var fingers = 0;
 1106:   bool disableScroll = false;
 1107: 
 1108:   int get preCacheCount => appdata.settings["preloadImageCount"];
 1109: 
 1110:   /// Image width as a fraction of the viewport height, applied when
 1111:   /// `limitImageWidth` is on. Replaces the old fixed 0.7 so a tall strip can be
 1112:   /// sized between the two former extremes instead of only fit-to-width or
 1113:   /// unconstrained. At the top of the range the cap exceeds the window's
 1114:   /// own ratio and stops applying, which is the unconstrained case.
 1115:   double get _imageWidthRatio {
 1116:     var value = appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 1117:       reader.cid,
 1118:       reader.type.sourceKey,
 1119:       'imageWidthPercent',
 1120:     );
 1121:     if (value is num) {
 1122:       return (value.toDouble() / 100).clamp(0.4, 1.5);
 1123:     }
 1124:     return 0.7;
 1125:   }
 1126: 
 1127:   /// Whether the user was scrolling the page.
 1128:   /// The gesture detector has a delay to detect tap event.
 1129:   /// To handle the tap event, we need to know if the user was scrolling before the delay.
 1130:   bool delayedIsScrolling = false;
 1131: 
 1132:   var imageStates = <State<ComicImage>>{};
 1133: 
 1134:   // Loaded chapter metadata. Do NOT evict this without preserving the
 1135:   // center-keyed slivers' visual offset (including RTL and comment footers).
 1136:   // Only prefetch bookkeeping below is a bounded moving window.
 1137:   final _continuousChapterImages = <int, List<String>>{};
 1138:   final _continuousChapterLoads = <int, Future<void>>{};
 1139:   final _continuousChapterErrors = <int, String>{};
 1140:   final _continuousCachedImages = <String>{};
 1141: 
 1142:   // This is bookkeeping, not the disk/image cache. A small recent-key FIFO
 1143:   // prevents session-long retention of URLs. In-flight requests are separately
 1144:   // coalesced by ImageDownloader's cache-key map even after FIFO eviction.
 1145:   int get _prefetchDedupLimit => math.max(32, preCacheCount * 4);
 1146: 
 1147:   bool _rememberPrefetch<T>(Set<T> keys, T key) {
 1148:     final limit = _prefetchDedupLimit;
 1149:     while (keys.length > limit) {
 1150:       keys.remove(keys.first);
 1151:     }
 1152:     if (keys.contains(key)) return false;
 1153:     if (keys.length == limit) keys.remove(keys.first);
 1154:     keys.add(key);
 1155:     return true;
 1156:   }
 1157: 
 1158:   void _reportSessionWork() {
 1159:     debugReaderContinuousSessionWork?.call(ReaderContinuousSessionWork(
 1160:       chapters: _continuousChapterImages.length,
 1161:       entries: _entries.length,
 1162:       indexedChapters: _chapterEntryRanges.length,
 1163:       prefetchKeys: _continuousCachedImages.length,
 1164:       singleChapterPrefetchKeys: _cachedPages.length,
 1165:       prefetchLimit: _prefetchDedupLimit,
 1166:     ));
 1167:   }
 1168:   late final ContinuousPageTurnCoordinator<_ContinuousReaderEntry>
 1169:   _pageTurnCoordinator;
 1170:   int _turnInteractionGeneration = 0;
 1171:   int? _boundaryTurnChapter;
 1172: 
 1173:   /// Pages already pre-downloaded in non-seamless (single-chapter) mode.
 1174:   final _cachedPages = <int>{};
 1175: 
 1176:   /// The chapter the reader was opened on. This, together with [_anchorPage],
 1177:   /// is the *pivot* of the center-keyed [CustomScrollView]: the pivot entry is
 1178:   /// laid out at scroll offset 0 and everything before it grows upward in a
 1179:   /// reverse sliver. Because the pivot is identified by (chapter, page) rather
 1180:   /// than by a list index, prepending an earlier chapter extends the reverse
 1181:   /// sliver without moving the pivot — so the viewport never jumps. This is the
 1182:   /// core fix for the "jump away then back" seen when a previous chapter loaded.
 1183:   late int _anchorChapter;
 1184:   late int _anchorPage;
 1185: 
 1186:   /// Flat, natural-order list of entries across all currently-loaded chapters,
 1187:   /// rebuilt only when the set of loaded chapters / their images change.
 1188:   List<_ContinuousReaderEntry> _entries = const [];
 1189: 
 1190:   // Image entries of one chapter are contiguous. Rebuilt atomically with
 1191:   // _entries, never maintained from reader.chapter (which changes on scroll).
 1192:   final _chapterEntryRanges = <int, ({int start, int length})>{};
 1193:   final _chapterSeparatorIndices = <int, int>{};
 1194: 
 1195:   /// Index into [_entries] of the pivot entry (the one carrying [_centerKey]).
 1196:   int _anchorIndex = 0;
 1197: 
 1198:   /// Center key handed to the [CustomScrollView]; marks the pivot sliver.
 1199:   /// Replaced together with [_pivotGeneration] whenever the pivot moves.
 1200:   GlobalKey _centerKey = GlobalKey();
 1201: 
 1202:   /// Bumped by [_repivot]. Both slivers are keyed on it so a pivot move
 1203:   /// rebuilds them instead of shifting every child's index under a live list.
 1204:   int _pivotGeneration = 0;
 1205: 
 1206:   /// Set while [_repivot] moves the offset to 0 ahead of the new layout;
 1207:   /// against the old extents that offset can read as the chapter start.
 1208:   bool _repivoting = false;
 1209: 
 1210:   /// 當前這次滾動是否由「使用者自己的拖動」發起。
 1211:   ///
 1212:   /// 程式化滾動（還原上次閱讀位置）和圖片載入造成的版面位移都不是，
 1213:   /// 它們以前會把 readerScrolling / lastScrollStop 設成當下，
 1214:   /// 導致剛進漫畫還沒動手就被 800ms 靜止判定擋住第一次點擊。
 1215:   bool _userDragging = false;

 1217:   /// Per-image GlobalKeys ("chapter:page") used to read each visible item's
 1218:   /// render box during scroll so we can resolve the current reading position
 1219:   /// without [ScrollablePositionedList]'s itemPositions listener.
 1220:   final _itemKeys = <String, GlobalKey>{};

 1225:   // Reading-order index of ACTIVE layout shells only. Maintenance happens on
 1226:   // lifecycle changes, never by sorting/scanning historical entries per frame.
 1227:   final _mountedGeometry = <_ContinuousImageGeometryState>[];
 1228:   _ContinuousReaderEntry? _firstImageEntry;
 1229:   int _geometryVisits = 0;
 1230: 
 1231:   void _registerGeometry(_ContinuousImageGeometryState node) {
 1232:     _mountedGeometry.remove(node);
 1233:     final entry = node.widget.entry;
 1234:     var low = 0;
 1235:     var high = _mountedGeometry.length;
 1236:     while (low < high) {
 1237:       final mid = (low + high) ~/ 2;
 1238:       final other = _mountedGeometry[mid].widget.entry;
 1239:       if (other.chapter < entry.chapter ||
 1240:           (other.chapter == entry.chapter && other.page < entry.page)) {
 1241:         low = mid + 1;
 1242:       } else {
 1243:         high = mid;
 1244:       }
 1245:     }
 1246:     _mountedGeometry.insert(low, node);
 1247:   }
 1248: 
 1249:   void _unregisterGeometry(_ContinuousImageGeometryState node) {
 1250:     // Remove by State identity, not chapter:page: an old lifecycle must never
 1251:     // unregister a replacement for the same entry.
 1252:     _mountedGeometry.remove(node);
 1253:   }
 1254: 
 1255:   Iterable<(_ContinuousReaderEntry, RenderBox)> _laidOutImages(
 1256:       {bool reverse = false}) sync* {
 1257:     final nodes = reverse ? _mountedGeometry.reversed : _mountedGeometry;
 1258:     for (final node in nodes) {
 1259:       _geometryVisits++;
 1260:       final box = node.context.findRenderObject();
 1261:       if (box is RenderBox && box.attached && box.hasSize) {
 1262:         yield (node.widget.entry, box);
 1263:       }
 1264:     }
 1265:   }
 1266: 
 1267:   void _rebuildEntries() {
 1268:     if (seamlessChapterReading) {
 1269:       _entries = _continuousEntries();
 1270:     } else {
 1271:       // Single-chapter continuous mode: just this chapter's pages. Chapter
 1272:       // changes happen via the edge-swipe gesture, which rebuilds the whole
 1273:       // widget with the new chapter — no separators or cross-chapter joining.
 1274:       final imgs = reader.images ?? const <String>[];
 1275:       _entries = [
 1276:         for (var i = 0; i < imgs.length; i++)
 1277:           _ContinuousReaderEntry.image(
 1278:             chapter: reader.chapter,
 1279:             page: i + 1,
 1280:             imageKey: imgs[i],
 1281:           ),
 1282:       ];
 1283:     }
 1284:     _chapterEntryRanges.clear();
 1285:     _chapterSeparatorIndices.clear();
 1286:     _firstImageEntry = null;
 1287:     for (var i = 0; i < _entries.length; i++) {
 1288:       final entry = _entries[i];
 1289:       if (entry.isImage) {
 1290:         _firstImageEntry ??= entry;
 1291:         final range = _chapterEntryRanges[entry.chapter];
 1292:         _chapterEntryRanges[entry.chapter] = (
 1293:           start: range?.start ?? i,
 1294:           length: (range?.length ?? 0) + 1,
 1295:         );
 1296:       } else {
 1297:         _chapterSeparatorIndices[entry.chapter] = i;
 1298:       }
 1299:     }
 1300:     _anchorIndex = _indexOfEntry(_anchorChapter, _anchorPage);
 1301:     _reportSessionWork();
 1302:     _lastCacheAroundChapter = -1;
 1303:     _lastCacheAroundPage = -1;
 1304:   }

 1308:       if (!mounted) {
 1309:         return;
 1310:       }
 1311:       delayedIsScrolling = value;
 1312:     });
 1313:   }
