 1401:   /// Builds the flat entry list in reading order, spanning every currently
 1402:   /// loaded chapter (earliest first). There is no leading spacer: index 0 is
 1403:   /// the first real entry. The pivot/center is chosen separately via
 1404:   /// [_indexOfEntry], without changing the chapters' original indices.
 1405:   List<_ContinuousReaderEntry> _continuousEntries() {
 1406:     if (reader.images != null &&
 1407:         !identical(_continuousChapterImages[reader.chapter], reader.images)) {
 1408:       _continuousChapterImages[reader.chapter] = reader.images!;
 1409:     }
 1410:     final entries = <_ContinuousReaderEntry>[];
 1411: 
 1412:     // Find the lowest consecutively loaded chapter at or below the anchor,
 1413:     // stepping over chapters hidden as duplicates.
 1414:     int lowestChapter = _anchorChapter;
 1415:     for (
 1416:       var ch = reader.visibleChapterFrom(_anchorChapter, -1);
 1417:       ch != null;
 1418:       ch = reader.visibleChapterFrom(ch, -1)
 1419:     ) {
 1420:       if (_continuousChapterImages.containsKey(ch)) {
 1421:         lowestChapter = ch;
 1422:       } else {
 1423:         break;
 1424:       }
 1425:     }
 1426: 
 1427:     // A "previous chapter" separator at the very top if earlier chapters exist.
 1428:     final prevChapter = reader.visibleChapterFrom(lowestChapter, -1);
 1429:     if (prevChapter != null) {
 1430:       entries.add(
 1431:         _ContinuousReaderEntry.separator(
 1432:           chapter: 0,
 1433:           hasNext: true,
 1434:           nextChapter: prevChapter,
 1435:           isLoading: _continuousChapterLoads.containsKey(prevChapter),
 1436:           error: _continuousChapterErrors[prevChapter],
 1437:         ),
 1438:       );
 1439:     }
 1440: 
 1441:     // Images from lowestChapter up through all consecutively loaded chapters,
 1442:     // skipping chapters hidden as duplicates.
 1443:     for (int? chapter = lowestChapter; chapter != null; ) {
 1444:       final images = _continuousChapterImages[chapter];
 1445:       if (images == null) {
 1446:         break;
 1447:       }
 1448:       for (var i = 0; i < images.length; i++) {
 1449:         entries.add(
 1450:           _ContinuousReaderEntry.image(
 1451:             chapter: chapter,
 1452:             page: i + 1,
 1453:             imageKey: images[i],
 1454:           ),
 1455: =======
 1456:         return _ContinuousMode(
 1457:           key: Key('${reader.mode.key}_${reader.showChapterCommentsAtEnd}'),
 1458: >>>>>>> synthetic/upstream-v3.0.0-walter-base
 1459:         );
 1460:       }
 1461:     }
 1462: <<<<<<< HEAD
 1463:     return entries;
--- conflict at lines 1462-3138 ---
 1457:           key: Key('${reader.mode.key}_${reader.showChapterCommentsAtEnd}'),
 1458: >>>>>>> synthetic/upstream-v3.0.0-walter-base
 1459:         );
 1460:       }
 1461:     }
 1462: <<<<<<< HEAD
 1463:     return entries;
 1464:   }
 1465: 
 1466:   /// Index into [_entries] of the image entry at (chapter, page), or the
 1467:   /// nearest valid index if not found.
 1468:   int _indexOfEntry(int chapter, int page) {
 1469:     final range = _chapterEntryRanges[chapter];
 1470:     debugReaderContinuousIndexProbes?.call(1);
 1471:     if (range == null) return 0;
 1472:     // Preserve the old exact-page / first-image-of-chapter fallback, including
 1473:     // page 0 and pages past the end. Separators never count as images.
 1474:     return range.start + (page >= 1 && page <= range.length ? page - 1 : 0);
 1475:   }
 1476: 
 1477:   Future<void> _ensureContinuousChapterLoaded(int chapter) {
 1478:     if (chapter < 1 ||
 1479:         chapter > reader.maxChapter ||
 1480:         _continuousChapterImages.containsKey(chapter)) {
 1481:       return Future.value();
 1482:     }
 1483:     final existing = _continuousChapterLoads[chapter];
 1484:     if (existing != null) {
 1485:       return existing;
 1486:     }
 1487:     // With the center-keyed CustomScrollView, an earlier chapter is prepended
 1488:     // into the reverse sliver that grows *away* from the pivot, so inserting it
 1489:     // does not move the pivot or any currently-visible content. No scroll
 1490:     // position compensation (and no "has the user scrolled yet" gate) is needed
 1491:     // — this is exactly the jump that the rewrite removes.
 1492:     final future = _loadContinuousChapterImages(chapter)
 1493:         .then((images) {
 1494:           if (!mounted) {
 1495:             return;
 1496:           }
 1497:           setState(() {
 1498:             _continuousChapterImages[chapter] = images;
 1499:             _continuousChapterErrors.remove(chapter);
 1500:             _rebuildEntries();
 1501:           });
 1502:           final currentIndex = _indexOfEntry(reader.chapter, reader.page);
 1503:           if (_entries.isNotEmpty && _entries[currentIndex].isImage) {
 1504:             _cacheAround(_entries[currentIndex]);
 1505:           }
 1506:         })
 1507:         .catchError((e, s) {
 1508:           Log.error('Continuous chapter reading', e, s);
 1509:           if (!mounted) {
 1510:             return;
 1511:           }
 1512:           setState(() {
 1513:             _continuousChapterErrors[chapter] = e.toString();
 1514:             _rebuildEntries();
 1515:           });
 1516:         })
 1517:         .whenComplete(() {
 1518:           _continuousChapterLoads.remove(chapter);
 1519:           if (mounted) {
 1520:             setState(_rebuildEntries);
 1521:           }
 1522:         });
 1523:     _continuousChapterLoads[chapter] = future;
 1524:     _rebuildEntries();
 1525:     return future;
 1526:   }
 1527: 
 1528:   Future<List<String>> _loadContinuousChapterImages(int chapter) => loadReaderChapter(
 1529:     prefetchComments: () => reader.prefetchChapterComments(chapter),
 1530:     loadImages: () => _loadContinuousImages(chapter),
 1531:   );
 1532: 
 1533:   Future<List<String>> _loadContinuousImages(int chapter) async {
 1534:     if (reader.type == ComicType.local ||
 1535:         LocalManager().isDownloaded(
 1536:           reader.cid,
 1537:           reader.type,
 1538:           chapter,
 1539:           reader.widget.chapters,
 1540:         )) {
 1541:       return LocalManager().getImages(reader.cid, reader.type, chapter);
 1542:     }
 1543:     final chapterId = reader.widget.chapters?.ids.elementAtOrNull(chapter - 1);
 1544:     final res = await reader.type.comicSource!.loadComicPages!(
 1545:       reader.widget.cid,
 1546:       chapterId,
 1547:     );
 1548:     if (res.error) {
 1549:       throw res.errorMessage ?? 'Failed to load next chapter';
 1550:     }
 1551:     return res.data;
 1552:   }
 1553: 
 1554:   void _syncReaderLocation(_ContinuousReaderEntry entry) {
 1555:     if (!entry.isImage) {
 1556:       return;
 1557:     }
 1558:     final images = _continuousChapterImages[entry.chapter];
 1559:     if (images == null) {
 1560:       return;
 1561:     }
 1562:     // Only rebuild the scaffold when the logical location actually changes.
 1563:     // The scroll listener fires at sub-frame frequency; calling
 1564:     // readerScaffold.update() (a full setState on the toolbar/battery/clock/
 1565:     // progress) on every tick was a steady source of dropped frames.
 1566:     if (reader.chapter != entry.chapter) {
 1567:       reader.continuousChapterEndReached = false;
 1568:       reader.chapter = entry.chapter;
 1569:       reader.images = images;
 1570:       reader.page = entry.page;
 1571:       context.readerScaffold.update();
 1572:     } else if (entry.page != reader.page) {
 1573:       reader.setPage(entry.page);
 1574:       context.readerScaffold.update();
 1575:     }
 1576:   }
 1577: 
 1578:   /// Whether the geometry walk is already scheduled for the next frame, so
 1579:   /// rapid scroll ticks coalesce into one resolution per frame.
 1580:   bool _positionResolveScheduled = false;
 1581: 
 1582:   void onScroll() {
 1583:     // Swipe-past-edge to change chapter only applies in non-seamless mode.
 1584:     if (!seamlessChapterReading) {
 1585:       _updateSwipeChangeChapter();
 1586:     }
 1587:     _schedulePositionResolve();
 1588:   }
 1589: 
 1590:   void _schedulePositionResolve() {
 1591:     if (_positionResolveScheduled) return;
 1592:     _positionResolveScheduled = true;
 1593:     WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
 1594:       _positionResolveScheduled = false;
 1595:       if (mounted) _onScrollPositionSettled();
 1596:     });
 1597:   }
 1598: 
 1599:   /// Resolves the current reading position from render-box geometry (replacing
 1600:   /// ScrollablePositionedList's itemPositions listener). Drives the scaffold
 1601:   /// page indicator for both modes, and chapter-window loading for seamless.
 1602:   void _onScrollPositionSettled() {
 1603:     final visitsBefore = _geometryVisits;
 1604:     final mountedImages = _mountedGeometry.length;
 1605:     try {
 1606:       _resolveScrollPosition();
 1607:     } finally {
 1608:       debugReaderContinuousGeometryWork?.call(ReaderContinuousGeometryWork(
 1609:         _entries.length, mountedImages, _geometryVisits - visitsBefore));
 1610:     }
 1611:   }
 1612: 
 1613:   void _resolveScrollPosition() {
 1614:     if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;
 1615:     final box = context.findRenderObject();
 1616:     if (box is! RenderBox) return;
 1617:     final viewportLeadingGlobal = box.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 1618:     final vertical = reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom;
 1619: 
 1620:     // Anchor line for resolving the current page. Normally the viewport's
 1621:     // leading edge (offset 1). But with center-on-turn on, a turned-to page is
 1622:     // parked at the viewport center, leaving the *previous* page straddling the
 1623:     // leading edge — resolving against the edge would write the page back to
 1624:     // the previous one, so the next tap re-centers the same target and the
 1625:     // reader looks stuck one page in (issue #117-2 regression). Probe the
 1626:     // center instead so the centered page resolves as current.
 1627:     final center =
 1628:         vertical &&
 1629:         appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 1630:               reader.cid,
 1631:               reader.type.sourceKey,
 1632:               'readerCenterPageOnTurn',
 1633:             ) ==
 1634:             true;
 1635:     final viewExtent = vertical ? box.size.height : box.size.width;
 1636:     final probe = center ? viewExtent / 2 : 1.0;
 1637: 
 1638:     _ContinuousReaderEntry? current;
 1639:     // Last image whose box lies entirely at/above the leading edge. When the
 1640:     // edge falls on a chapter separator (a mid-chapter join page or the final
 1641:     // "no next chapter" page) no image straddles it, and the next image begins
 1642:     // below it. Snapping to that next image would jump the indicator to the
 1643:     // following chapter's P1 (or, at the tail, back to the very first image);
 1644:     // instead we keep the finished chapter's last page so the read-complete
 1645:     // mark can appear (issue #115, seamless case).
 1646:     _ContinuousReaderEntry? lastAbove;
 1647:     // Find the image entry whose box straddles the viewport's leading edge.
 1648:     for (final (entry, itemBox) in _laidOutImages()) {
 1649:       final topLeft = itemBox.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 1650:       final extent = vertical ? itemBox.size.height : itemBox.size.width;
 1651:       // Logical distance from the reading-leading edge: right edge for RTL.
 1652:       // Entry order is reading order even when physical x decreases.
 1653:       final start = vertical
 1654:           ? topLeft.dy - viewportLeadingGlobal.dy
 1655:           : _reverse
 1656:               ? viewportLeadingGlobal.dx + box.size.width -
 1657:                   (topLeft.dx + extent)
 1658:               : topLeft.dx - viewportLeadingGlobal.dx;
 1659:       // The entry crossing the anchor line: starts at/above it and ends below.
 1660:       if (start <= probe && start + extent > probe) {
 1661:         current = entry;
 1662:         break;
 1663:       }
 1664:       if (start <= probe) {
 1665:         // Wholly above the anchor; remember as the running "last above".
 1666:         lastAbove = entry;
 1667:         continue;
 1668:       }
 1669:       // start > probe: first image beginning below the anchor. If an earlier
 1670:       // image sits above it, a separator holds the anchor between them — stay
 1671:       // on that finished page rather than advancing into the page below.
 1672:       current = lastAbove ?? entry;
 1673:       break;
 1674:     }
 1675:     current ??= lastAbove;
 1676:     // Final image numbering still snaps at the complete scroll tail, not at
 1677:     // the last image's bottom. Completion separately checks the footer below.
 1678:     // This retains the short-final-image position fix without early completion.
 1679:     final pos = _scrollController.position;
 1680:     if (pos.hasContentDimensions &&
 1681:         pos.maxScrollExtent > pos.minScrollExtent &&
 1682:         pos.pixels >= pos.maxScrollExtent - 1) {
 1683:       current = _lastLaidOutImageEntry() ?? current;
 1684:     }
 1685:     current ??= _firstImageEntry;
 1686:     if (current == null) return;
 1687:     if (!current.isImage) return;
 1688:     // A seamless window can already contain the next chapter's images. Do
 1689:     // not let a drag/ticker resolve into them across an unresolved footer.
 1690:     if (current.chapter > reader.chapter &&
 1691:         !reader.chapterCommentsSettled(reader.chapter)) {
 1692:       final footer = _footerKeys[reader.chapter]?.currentContext?.findRenderObject();
 1693:       if (footer is RenderBox && footer.attached) {
 1694:         final origin = footer.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 1695:         final end = vertical
 1696:             ? origin.dy - viewportLeadingGlobal.dy + footer.size.height
 1697:             : _reverse
 1698:                 ? viewportLeadingGlobal.dx + box.size.width - origin.dx
 1699:                 : origin.dx - viewportLeadingGlobal.dx + footer.size.width;
 1700:         final target = (pos.pixels + end - viewExtent)
 1701:             .clamp(pos.minScrollExtent, pos.maxScrollExtent);
 1702:         if ((target - pos.pixels).abs() > 1) _scrollController.jumpTo(target);
 1703:       }
 1704:       return;
 1705:     }
 1706:     _syncReaderLocation(current);
 1707:     final reached = !showChapterCommentsAtEnd || _footerReached(reader.chapter);
 1708:     if (reader.continuousChapterEndReached != reached) {
 1709:       reader.continuousChapterEndReached = reached;
 1710:       reader.updateHistory();
 1711:     }
 1712:     if (seamlessChapterReading) {
 1713:       _maybeLoadAroundCurrent(current);
 1714:       _cacheAround(current);
 1715:     } else {
 1716:       cacheImages(current.page);
 1717:     }
 1718:   }
 1719: 
 1720:   /// Loads the previous/next chapter as the current reading position
 1721:   /// approaches a chapter boundary.
 1722:   void _maybeLoadAroundCurrent(_ContinuousReaderEntry current) {
 1723:     final chapterImages = _continuousChapterImages[current.chapter];
 1724:     if (chapterImages == null) return;
 1725:     const edge = 3; // pages from the boundary that trigger a window slide
 1726:     // Near the end -> ensure next chapter.
 1727:     if (current.page >= chapterImages.length - edge) {
 1728:       final next = reader.visibleChapterFrom(current.chapter, 1);
 1729:       if (next != null) {
 1730:         _ensureContinuousChapterLoaded(next);
 1731:       }
 1732:     }
 1733:     // Near the start -> ensure previous chapter.
 1734:     if (current.page <= edge + 1) {
 1735:       final prev = reader.visibleChapterFrom(current.chapter, -1);
 1736:       if (prev != null) {
 1737:         _ensureContinuousChapterLoaded(prev);
 1738:       }
 1739:     }
 1740:   }
 1741: 
 1742:   double? _futurePosition;
 1743: 
 1744:   void smoothTo(double offset) {
 1745:     if (HardwareKeyboard.instance.isShiftPressed) {
 1746:       return;
 1747:     }
 1748:     // A wheel/key event can arrive in the same frame the reader is torn down
 1749:     // (chapter remount, back navigation). Reading position/animating a
 1750:     // detached controller throws; the scroll itself is already moot.
 1751:     if (!mounted || !scrollController.hasClients) {
 1752:       return;
 1753:     }
 1754:     var currentLocation = scrollController.position.pixels;
 1755:     var old = _futurePosition;
 1756:     _futurePosition ??= currentLocation;
 1757:     double k = (_futurePosition! - currentLocation).abs() / 1600 + 1;
 1758:     final customSpeed = appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 1759:       context.reader.cid,
 1760:       context.reader.type.sourceKey,
 1761:       "readerScrollSpeed",
 1762:     );
 1763:     if (customSpeed is num) {
 1764:       k *= customSpeed;
 1765:     }
 1766:     _futurePosition = _futurePosition! + offset * k;
 1767:     var beforeOffset = (_futurePosition! - currentLocation).abs();
 1768:     _futurePosition = _futurePosition!.clamp(
 1769:       scrollController.position.minScrollExtent,
 1770:       scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent,
 1771:     );
 1772:     var afterOffset = (_futurePosition! - currentLocation).abs();
 1773:     if (_futurePosition == old) return;
 1774:     var target = _futurePosition!;
 1775:     var duration = const Duration(milliseconds: 160);
 1776:     if (afterOffset < beforeOffset) {
 1777:       duration = duration * (afterOffset / beforeOffset);
 1778:       if (duration < Duration(milliseconds: 10)) {
 1779:         duration = Duration(milliseconds: 10);
 1780:       }
 1781:     }
 1782:     scrollController
 1783:         .animateTo(_futurePosition!, duration: duration, curve: Curves.linear)
 1784:         .then((_) {
 1785:           // animateTo completes after a dispose/remount too; the position
 1786:           // assertion would then escape as an unhandled async error.
 1787:           if (!mounted || !scrollController.hasClients) return;
 1788:           var current = scrollController.position.pixels;
 1789:           if (current == target && current == _futurePosition) {
 1790:             _futurePosition = null;
 1791:           }
 1792:         });
 1793:   }
 1794: 
 1795:   void onPointerSignal(PointerSignalEvent event) {
 1796:     if (event is PointerScrollEvent) {
 1797:       if (!_isMouseScrolling) {
 1798:         setState(() {
 1799:           _isMouseScrolling = true;
 1800:         });
 1801:       }
 1802:       if (isCTRLPressed) {
 1803:         return;
 1804:       }
 1805:       _cancelProgrammaticPageTurn();
 1806:       smoothTo(event.scrollDelta.dy);
 1807:     }
 1808:   }
 1809: 
 1810:   void _cancelProgrammaticPageTurn() {
 1811:     _turnInteractionGeneration++;
 1812:     _boundaryTurnChapter = null;
 1813:     _pageTurnCoordinator.cancel();
 1814:   }
 1815: 
 1816:   /// Entry [_cacheAround] last ran for; the scroll listener settles every
 1817:   /// frame, and the entry list scan is wasted while the page has not moved.
 1818:   int _lastCacheAroundChapter = -1;
 1819:   int _lastCacheAroundPage = -1;
 1820: 
 1821:   /// Pre-download (never decode) around the current entry in seamless mode.
 1822:   ///
 1823:   /// Decoding is left to the sliver's cacheExtent through [ComicImage], whose
 1824:   /// scroll-aware provider holds off during fast flings. Decoding here via
 1825:   /// [precacheImage] bypassed that and ran mid-fling on every page the window
 1826:   /// slid over, which is what made seamless mode feel choppier (issue #260).
 1827:   void _cacheAround(_ContinuousReaderEntry current) {
 1828:     if (current.chapter == _lastCacheAroundChapter &&
 1829:         current.page == _lastCacheAroundPage) {
 1830:       return;
 1831:     }
 1832:     _lastCacheAroundChapter = current.chapter;
 1833:     _lastCacheAroundPage = current.page;
 1834:     final idx = _indexOfEntry(current.chapter, current.page);
 1835:     var remaining = preCacheCount;
 1836:     for (var i = idx + 1; i < _entries.length && remaining > 0; i++) {
 1837:       final entry = _entries[i];
 1838:       if (!entry.isImage) continue;
 1839:       remaining--;
 1840:       final cacheKey = '${entry.chapter}:${entry.page}:${entry.imageKey}';
 1841:       if (_rememberPrefetch(_continuousCachedImages, cacheKey)) {
 1842:         _preDownloadImageEntry(entry, context);
 1843:       }
 1844:     }
 1845:     remaining = preCacheCount;
 1846:     for (var i = idx - 1; i >= 0 && remaining > 0; i--) {
 1847:       final entry = _entries[i];
 1848:       if (!entry.isImage) continue;
 1849:       remaining--;
 1850:       final cacheKey = '${entry.chapter}:${entry.page}:${entry.imageKey}';
 1851:       if (_rememberPrefetch(_continuousCachedImages, cacheKey)) {
 1852:         _preDownloadImageEntry(entry, context);
 1853:       }
 1854:     }
 1855:     _reportSessionWork();
 1856:   }
 1857: 
 1858:   /// Pre-download around [current] page in non-seamless (single-chapter) mode.
 1859:   void cacheImages(int current) {
 1860:     for (int i = current + 1; i <= current + preCacheCount; i++) {
 1861:       if (i >= 1 && i <= reader.maxPage && _rememberPrefetch(_cachedPages, i)) {
 1862:         _preDownloadImage(i, context);
 1863:       }
 1864:     }
 1865:     _reportSessionWork();
 1866:   }
 1867: 
 1868:   void _updateSwipeChangeChapter() {
 1869:     final rawOffset = appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 1870:       reader.cid,
 1871:       reader.type.sourceKey,
 1872:       'changeChapterOffset',
 1873:     );
 1874:     final changeOffset = (rawOffset is num && rawOffset > 0)
 1875:         ? rawOffset.toDouble()
 1876:         : _kChangeChapterOffset;
 1877:     if (prepareToPrevChapter) {
 1878:       jumpToNextChapter = false;
 1879:       final passed =
 1880:           scrollController.offset <
 1881:           scrollController.position.minScrollExtent - changeOffset;
 1882:       // 跨過門檻的一瞬間震一下：此前的「上滑切下一章 / 下滑切上一章」
 1883:       // 只有視覺進度條，完全沒有觸覺反饋。
 1884:       if (passed && !jumpToPrevChapter) {
 1885:         HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
 1886:       }
 1887:       jumpToPrevChapter = passed;
 1888:     } else if (prepareToNextChapter) {
 1889:       final passed =
 1890:           scrollController.offset >
 1891:           scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent + changeOffset;
 1892:       if (passed && !jumpToNextChapter) {
 1893:         HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
 1894:       }
 1895:       jumpToNextChapter = passed;
 1896:       jumpToPrevChapter = false;
 1897:     }
 1898:   }
 1899: 
 1900:   bool onScaleUpdate([double? scale]) {
 1901:     if (prepareToNextChapter || prepareToPrevChapter) {
 1902:       setState(() {
 1903:         prepareToPrevChapter = false;
 1904:         prepareToNextChapter = false;
 1905:       });
 1906:       context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(0);
 1907:     }
 1908:     var isZoomedIn = (scale ?? photoViewController.scale) != 1.0;
 1909:     if (isZoomedIn != this.isZoomedIn) {
 1910:       setState(() {
 1911:         this.isZoomedIn = isZoomedIn;
 1912:       });
 1913:     }
 1914:     return false;
 1915:   }
 1916: 
 1917:   Widget _buildChapterJoinPage(
 1918:     BuildContext context,
 1919:     _ContinuousReaderEntry entry,
 1920:   ) {
 1921:     // chapter == 0 means this is a "previous chapter" separator at the top
 1922:     final isPrevChapterSeparator = entry.chapter == 0;
 1923:     final title = !entry.hasNext
 1924:         ? 'No next chapter'.tl
 1925:         : isPrevChapterSeparator
 1926:         ? 'Previous Chapter'.tl
 1927:         : 'Next Chapter'.tl;
 1928:     final subtitle = entry.hasNext && entry.nextChapter != null
 1929:         ? _chapterTitle(entry.nextChapter!)
 1930:         : reader.widget.name;
 1931:     final status = entry.error != null
 1932:         ? 'Tap to retry'.tl
 1933:         : entry.isLoading
 1934:         ? 'Loading'.tl
 1935:         : null;
 1936:     // Not reader.size: that reads the RenderBox during build, so it returns the
 1937:     // previous layout's size and stays stale after a rotation.
 1938:     final viewportSize = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
 1939:     return ColoredBox(
 1940:       color: reader.readerBackgroundColor,
 1941:       child: SizedBox(
 1942:         width: viewportSize.width,
 1943:         height: viewportSize.height,
 1944:         child: Center(
 1945:           child: InkWell(
 1946:             borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
 1947:             onTap: entry.hasNext && entry.nextChapter != null
 1948:                 ? () {
 1949:                     setState(() {
 1950:                       _continuousChapterErrors.remove(entry.nextChapter);
 1951:                       _rebuildEntries();
 1952:                     });
 1953:                     _ensureContinuousChapterLoaded(entry.nextChapter!);
 1954:                   }
 1955:                 : null,
 1956:             child: Padding(
 1957:               padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32, vertical: 28),
 1958:               child: Column(
 1959:                 mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
 1960:                 children: [
 1961:                   Icon(
 1962:                     !entry.hasNext
 1963:                         ? Icons.done_all_rounded
 1964:                         : isPrevChapterSeparator
 1965:                         ? Icons.keyboard_arrow_up_rounded
 1966:                         : Icons.keyboard_arrow_down_rounded,
 1967:                     size: 42,
 1968:                     color: context.colorScheme.primary,
 1969:                   ),
 1970:                   const SizedBox(height: 12),
 1971:                   Text(title, style: ts.s18.bold, textAlign: TextAlign.center),
 1972:                   const SizedBox(height: 8),
 1973:                   Text(
 1974:                     subtitle,
 1975:                     style: ts.s14.withColor(context.colorScheme.outline),
 1976:                     textAlign: TextAlign.center,
 1977:                     maxLines: 2,
 1978:                     overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
 1979:                   ),
 1980:                   if (status != null) ...[
 1981:                     const SizedBox(height: 12),
 1982:                     Text(
 1983:                       status,
 1984:                       style: ts.s12.withColor(
 1985:                         entry.error != null
 1986:                             ? context.colorScheme.error
 1987:                             : context.colorScheme.outline,
 1988:                       ),
 1989:                     ),
 1990:                   ],
 1991:                 ],
 1992:               ),
 1993:             ),
 1994:           ),
 1995:         ),
 1996:       ),
 1997:     );
 1998:   }

 2000:   /// Builds a single image entry widget, tagged with its position key so the
 2001:   /// scroll listener can read its render box.
 2002:   Widget _buildImageEntry(_ContinuousReaderEntry entry) {
 2003:     double? width, height;
 2004:     if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousLeftToRight ||
 2005:         reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousRightToLeft) {
 2006:       height = double.infinity;
 2007:     } else {
 2008:       width = double.infinity;
 2009:     }
 2010:     final image = _createImageProviderFromKey(
 2011:       entry.imageKey!,
 2012:       context,
 2013:       entry.page,
 2014:       chapter: entry.chapter,
 2015:     );
 2016:     return _ContinuousImageGeometry(
 2017:       key: _itemKeyFor(entry.chapter, entry.page),
 2018:       owner: this,
 2019:       entry: entry,
 2020:       child: ColoredBox(
 2021:         color: reader.readerBackgroundColor,
 2022:         child: ComicImage(
 2023:           filterQuality: FilterQuality.medium,
 2024:           image: image,
 2025:           width: width,
 2026:           height: height,
 2027:           fit: BoxFit.contain,
 2028:           onInit: (state) => imageStates.add(state),
 2029:           onDispose: (state) => imageStates.remove(state),
 2030:         ),
 2031:       ),
 2032:     );
 2033:   }
 2034: 
 2035:   /// Builds the widget for a flat entry (image, chapter-join separator, or a
 2036:   /// non-seamless single page).
 2037:   Widget _buildEntry(_ContinuousReaderEntry entry) {
 2038:     if (entry.isSeparator) {
 2039:       if (entry.hasNext && entry.nextChapter != null) {
 2040:         Future.microtask(
 2041:           () => _ensureContinuousChapterLoaded(entry.nextChapter!),
 2042:         );
 2043:       }
 2044:       final children = <Widget>[
 2045:         if (entry.chapter > 0 && reader.chapterCommentsEnabled)
 2046:           _buildChapterCommentsBlock(chapter: entry.chapter),
 2047:         _buildChapterJoinPage(context, entry),
 2048:       ];
 2049:       return _axis == Axis.vertical ? Column(children:children)
 2050:           : Row(textDirection:_reverse ? TextDirection.rtl : TextDirection.ltr,
 2051:               children:children);
 2052:     }
 2053:     Widget child = _buildImageEntry(entry);
 2054:     // 相邻图片之间的可选间隙(issue #117-3)。沿主轴在图片后加内边距，间隙区域
 2055:     // 由外层 ColoredBox 背景色填充。仅图片条目加，衔接页不受影响。
 2056:     final spacing =
 2057:         (appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 2058:                   reader.cid,
 2059:                   reader.type.sourceKey,
 2060:                   'readerPageSpacing',
 2061:                 )
 2062:                 as num?)
 2063:             ?.toDouble() ??
 2064:         0.0;
 2065:     if (spacing > 0) {
 2066:       child = reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom
 2067:           ? child.paddingBottom(spacing)
 2068:           : child.paddingRight(spacing);
 2069:     }
 2070:     return child;
 2071:   }
 2072: 
 2073:   ScrollPhysics get _physics =>
 2074:       isCTRLPressed || _isMouseScrolling || disableScroll
 2075:       ? const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics()
 2076:       : isZoomedIn
 2077:       ? const ClampingScrollPhysics()
 2078:       : const BouncingScrollPhysics(parent: AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics());
 2079: 
 2080:   ScrollBehavior get _scrollBehavior => const MaterialScrollBehavior().copyWith(
 2081:     scrollbars: false,
 2082:     dragDevices: _kTouchLikeDeviceTypes,
 2083:   );
 2084: 
 2085:   Axis get _axis => reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom
 2086:       ? Axis.vertical
 2087:       : Axis.horizontal;
 2088: 
 2089:   bool get _reverse => reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousRightToLeft;
 2090: 
 2091:   /// Center-keyed scroll view used by both seamless and single-chapter modes.
 2092:   ///
 2093:   /// The pivot entry ([_anchorIndex]) carries [_centerKey]. Entries *before*
 2094:   /// the pivot live in the first sliver, which (being before center) is laid
 2095:   /// out toward the leading edge; entries from the pivot onward live in the
 2096:   /// second sliver after the center. Two payoffs:
 2097:   ///  - Opening at a restored page lands exactly on it (the pivot sits at
 2098:   ///    offset 0) regardless of the variable image heights above it.
 2099:   ///  - Prepending an earlier chapter only grows the first sliver away from the
 2100:   ///    pivot, so the content the user is looking at stays pinned — no jump.
 2101:   /// ⑧ 連續滾動模式：滑過最後一頁後直接往下滑就能看到本章評論，
 2102:   /// 不必再點右上角的評論按鈕。
 2103:   bool get showChapterCommentsAtEnd {
 2104:     if (!reader.chapterCommentsEnabled) return false;
 2105:     var source = ComicSource.find(reader.type.sourceKey);
 2106:     if (source?.hasChapterComments != true || !reader.chapterCommentsAvailable(reader.eid)) return false;
 2107:     return appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 2108:               reader.cid,
 2109:               reader.type.sourceKey,
 2110:               'showChapterComments',
 2111:             ) ==
 2112:             true &&
 2113:         appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 2114:               reader.cid,
 2115:               reader.type.sourceKey,
 2116:               'showChapterCommentsAtEnd',
 2117:             ) ==
 2118:             true;
 2119:   }
 2120: 
 2121:   final Map<int, GlobalKey> _footerKeys = {};
 2122: 
 2123:   /// Whether this chapter actually renders a comments footer right now.
 2124:   /// The key map outlives a footer that a confirmed-empty/unavailable feed
 2125:   /// removed, so presence must come from the live element, not the map entry.
 2126:   bool _footerPresent(int chapter) {
 2127:     final context = _footerKeys[chapter]?.currentContext;
 2128:     final box = context?.findRenderObject();
 2129:     return box is RenderBox && box.attached && box.hasSize;
 2130:   }
 2131: 
 2132:   bool _footerReached(int chapter) {
 2133:     final box = _footerKeys[chapter]?.currentContext?.findRenderObject();
 2134:     final viewport = context.findRenderObject();
 2135:     if (box is! RenderBox || viewport is! RenderBox || !box.attached) return false;
 2136:     final origin = viewport.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 2137:     final trailing = box.localToGlobal(Offset(box.size.width,box.size.height));
 2138:     final leading = box.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 2139:     return readerFooterReachedEnd(
 2140:       footer:Rect.fromPoints(leading,trailing),
 2141:       viewport:origin & viewport.size,axis:_axis,reverse:_reverse);
 2142:   }
 2143: 
 2144:   Widget _buildChapterCommentsBlock({int? chapter}) {
 2145:     var source = ComicSource.find(reader.type.sourceKey);
 2146:     var chapters = reader.widget.chapters;
 2147:     if (source == null || chapters == null) return const SizedBox();
 2148:     var chapterIndex = (chapter ?? reader.chapter) - 1;
 2149:     if (chapterIndex < 0 || chapterIndex >= chapters.length) {
 2150:       return const SizedBox();
 2151:     }
 2152:     if (!reader.chapterCommentsAvailable(chapters.ids.elementAt(chapterIndex))) return const SizedBox.shrink();
 2153:     return ChapterCommentsPreview(
 2154:         key: _footerKeys.putIfAbsent(chapterIndex + 1, () => GlobalKey()),
 2155:         horizontal: _axis == Axis.horizontal,
 2156:         reverse: _reverse,
 2157:         comicId: reader.cid,
 2158:         epId: chapters.ids.elementAt(chapterIndex),
 2159:         source: source,
 2160:         comicTitle: reader.widget.name,
 2161:         chapterTitle: chapters.titles.elementAt(chapterIndex),
 2162:         sharedFeed: reader.chapterCommentsFeed(source, chapters.ids.elementAt(chapterIndex)),
 2163:     );
 2164:   }
 2165: 
 2166:   Widget _buildScrollView() {
 2167:     final before = _anchorIndex; // entries strictly before the pivot
 2168:     var afterCount = _entries.length - _anchorIndex; // pivot + following
 2169:     // Keep preloaded next chapters out of the scrollable range while the
 2170:     // current first page is unresolved (or awaiting retry). The separator and
 2171:     // its real footer remain reachable; no fabricated empty result is used.
 2172:     if (!reader.chapterCommentsSettled(reader.chapter)) {
 2173:       final boundary = _chapterSeparatorIndices[reader.chapter];
 2174:       if (boundary != null && boundary >= _anchorIndex) {
 2175:         afterCount = boundary - _anchorIndex + 1;
 2176:       }
 2177:     }
 2178:     // 连续滚动模式的卡顿主因：cacheExtent 决定 SliverList 在视口外提前多远
 2179:     // 构建并解码 item。原值仅 1.5 个视口，而漫画竖图常达 1~3 屏高，往往连下一
 2180:     // 张完整图都覆盖不到 —— 该图滚入视口时才在主 isolate 即时解码大图，导致掉帧
 2181:     // (issue #32 上下滚动卡顿)。按用户的预加载页数放大提前量(默认4→4屏)，
 2182:     // 并设 2 屏下限，使后续若干长图在进入视口前已解码就绪。continuous 模式
 2183:     // enableResize=true 已降采样，单张解码成本可控，放大提前量代价主要是内存，
 2184:     // 由 imageCache 的 LRU(按可用RAM 100~500MB)约束。
 2185:     final viewExtent = _axis == Axis.vertical
 2186:         ? reader.size.height
 2187:         : reader.size.width;
 2188:     final cacheExtent = viewExtent * preCacheCount.clamp(2, 6).toDouble();
 2189:     return CustomScrollView(
 2190:       controller: _scrollController,
 2191:       center: _centerKey,
 2192:       scrollDirection: _axis,
 2193:       reverse: _reverse,
 2194:       physics: _physics,
 2195:       scrollBehavior: _scrollBehavior,
 2196:       anchor: 0.0,
 2197:       scrollCacheExtent: ScrollCacheExtent.pixels(cacheExtent),
 2198:       slivers: [
 2199:         // Leading sliver: entries before the pivot, in reverse so element 0 of
 2200:         // the builder is the entry immediately above the pivot.
 2201:         SliverList(
 2202:           key: ValueKey('lead$_pivotGeneration'),
 2203:           delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
 2204:             (context, i) => _buildEntry(_entries[before - 1 - i]),
 2205:             childCount: before,
 2206:             addAutomaticKeepAlives: false,
 2207:             addSemanticIndexes: false,
 2208:           ),
 2209:         ),
 2210:         // Trailing sliver (the center): pivot entry and everything after it.
 2211:         SliverList(
 2212:           key: _centerKey,
 2213:           delegate: SliverChildBuilderDelegate(
 2214:             (context, i) => _buildEntry(_entries[before + i]),
 2215:             childCount: afterCount,
 2216:             addAutomaticKeepAlives: false,
 2217:             addSemanticIndexes: false,
 2218:           ),
 2219:         ),
 2220:         // ⑧ 章節末評論：下滑即見（可在閱讀設定關閉）。
 2221:         if (!seamlessChapterReading && showChapterCommentsAtEnd)
 2222:           SliverToBoxAdapter(child: _buildChapterCommentsBlock()),
 2223:       ],
 2224:     );
 2225:   }
 2226: 
 2227:   @override
 2228:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
 2229:     Widget widget = NotificationListener<ScrollMetricsNotification>(
 2230:       onNotification: (_) { _schedulePositionResolve(); return false; },
 2231:       child:NotificationListener<ScrollNotification>(
 2232:         onNotification: _onAutoScrollNotification,
 2233:         child: _buildScrollView(),
 2234:       ),
 2235:     );
 2236: 
 2237:     widget = Stack(
 2238:       children: [
 2239:         Positioned.fill(child: buildBackground(context)),
 2240:         Positioned.fill(child: widget),
 2241:       ],
 2242:     );
 2243: 
 2244:     // Pointer handling wraps PhotoView instead of living inside its child:
 2245:     // capping the image width letterboxes that child, and handlers confined to
 2246:     // it leave the margins dead to the wheel and trackpad.
 2247:     Widget buildPointerLayer(Widget child) => Listener(
 2248:       behavior: HitTestBehavior.translucent,
 2249:       onPointerDown: (event) {
 2250:         _increaseFingers();
 2251:         if (fingers > 1 && !disableScroll) {
 2252:           setState(() {
 2253:             disableScroll = true;
 2254:           });
 2255:         }
 2256:         _futurePosition = null;
 2257:         if (_isMouseScrolling) {
 2258:           setState(() {
 2259:             _isMouseScrolling = false;
 2260:           });
 2261:         }
 2262:       },
 2263:       onPointerUp: (event) {
 2264:         _decreaseFingers();
 2265:         if (fingers <= 1 && disableScroll) {
 2266:           setState(() {
 2267:             disableScroll = false;
 2268:           });
 2269:         }
 2270:         if (!seamlessChapterReading && fingers == 0) {
 2271:           if (jumpToPrevChapter) {
 2272:             context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(0);
 2273:             reader.toPrevChapter(toLastPage: true);
 2274:           } else if (jumpToNextChapter) {
 2275:             // The swipe already crossed maxScrollExtent. Requiring
 2276:             // chapterEndReached here made a pending/errored comments footer
 2277:             // silently drop the chapter turn.
 2278:             context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(0);
 2279:             reader.toNextChapter();
 2280:           }
 2281:         }
 2282:       },
 2283:       onPointerCancel: (event) {
 2284:         _decreaseFingers();
 2285:         if (fingers <= 1 && disableScroll) {
 2286:           setState(() {
 2287:             disableScroll = false;
 2288:           });
 2289:         }
 2290:       },
 2291:       onPointerPanZoomUpdate: (event) {
 2292:         if (event.scale == 1.0) {
 2293:           _cancelProgrammaticPageTurn();
 2294:           smoothTo(0 - event.panDelta.dy);
 2295:         }
 2296:       },
 2297:       onPointerMove: (event) {
 2298:         Offset value = event.delta;
 2299:         if (photoViewController.scale == 1 || fingers != 1) {
 2300:           return;
 2301:         }
 2302:         Offset offset;
 2303:         var sp = scrollController.position;
 2304:         if (sp.pixels <= sp.minScrollExtent ||
 2305:             sp.pixels >= sp.maxScrollExtent) {
 2306:           offset = Offset(value.dx, value.dy);
 2307:         } else {
 2308:           if (reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom) {
 2309:             offset = Offset(value.dx, 0);
 2310:           } else {
 2311:             offset = Offset(0, value.dy);
 2312:           }
 2313:         }
 2314:         if (isLongPressing) {
 2315:           offset += value;
 2316:         }
 2317:         photoViewController.updateMultiple(
 2318:           position: photoViewController.position + offset,
 2319:         );
 2320:       },
 2321:       onPointerSignal: onPointerSignal,
 2322:       child: child,
 2323:     );
 2324: 
 2325:     widget = NotificationListener<ScrollNotification>(
 2326:       onNotification: (notification) {
 2327:         if (_repivoting) return true;
 2328:         if (notification is ScrollStartNotification) {
 2329:           // 只有使用者真的拖動才算「滾動中」：初始定位／圖片載入造成的
 2330:           // 版面位移不該讓閱讀器看起來「剛停手」。
 2331:           if (notification.dragDetails != null) {
 2332:             _userDragging = true;
 2333:             reader.readerScrolling = true;
 2334:           }
 2335:           delayedSetIsScrolling(true);
 2336:           if (notification.dragDetails != null) {
 2337:             _cancelProgrammaticPageTurn();
 2338:           }
 2339:         } else if (notification is ScrollEndNotification) {
 2340:           if (_userDragging) {
 2341:             _userDragging = false;
 2342:             reader.readerScrolling = false;
 2343:             reader.lastScrollStop = DateTime.now();
 2344:           }
 2345:           delayedSetIsScrolling(false);
 2346:         }
 2347: 
 2348:         var scale = photoViewController.scale ?? 1.0;
 2349: 
 2350:         if (!seamlessChapterReading &&
 2351:             notification is ScrollUpdateNotification &&
 2352:             (scale - 1).abs() < 0.05) {
 2353:           if (!scrollController.hasClients) return false;
 2354:           if (scrollController.position.pixels <=
 2355:                   scrollController.position.minScrollExtent &&
 2356:               (scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent >
 2357:                       scrollController.position.minScrollExtent ||
 2358:                   scrollController.position.pixels <
 2359:                       scrollController.position.minScrollExtent) &&
 2360:               !reader.isFirstChapterOfGroup) {
 2361:             if (!prepareToPrevChapter) {
 2362:               jumpToPrevChapter = false;
 2363:               jumpToNextChapter = false;
 2364:               context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(-1);
 2365:               // 滑到章首、出現「下滑查看上一章」提示時給一次輕微觸覺。
 2366:               HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
 2367:               setState(() {
 2368:                 prepareToPrevChapter = true;
 2369:               });
 2370:             }
 2371:           } else if (scrollController.position.pixels >=
 2372:                   scrollController.position.maxScrollExtent &&
 2373:               !reader.isLastChapterOfGroup) {
 2374:             if (!prepareToNextChapter) {
 2375:               jumpToPrevChapter = false;
 2376:               jumpToNextChapter = false;
 2377:               context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(1);
 2378:               // 滑到章末、出現「上滑查看下一章」提示時給一次輕微觸覺。
 2379:               HapticFeedback.selectionClick();
 2380:               setState(() {
 2381:                 prepareToNextChapter = true;
 2382:               });
 2383:             }
 2384:           } else {
 2385:             context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(0);
 2386:             if (prepareToPrevChapter || prepareToNextChapter) {
 2387:               jumpToPrevChapter = false;
 2388:               jumpToNextChapter = false;
 2389:               setState(() {
 2390:                 prepareToPrevChapter = false;
 2391:                 prepareToNextChapter = false;
 2392:               });
 2393:             }
 2394:           }
 2395:         }
 2396: 
 2397:         return true;
 2398:       },
 2399:       child: widget,
 2400:     );
 2401:     final viewportSize = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
 2402:     var width = viewportSize.width;
 2403:     var height = viewportSize.height;
 2404:     // The ratio drives both the trigger and the cap: comparing against a fixed
 2405:     // 0.7 while capping at a larger ratio would widen the image past the
 2406:     // viewport it was meant to narrow.
 2407:     final widthRatio = _imageWidthRatio;
 2408:     if (appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 2409:               reader.cid,
 2410:               reader.type.sourceKey,
 2411:               'limitImageWidth',
 2412:             ) ==
 2413:             true &&
 2414:         width / height > widthRatio &&
 2415:         reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom) {
 2416:       width = height * widthRatio;
 2417:     }
 2418: 
 2419:     return buildPointerLayer(
 2420:       PhotoView.customChild(
 2421:         backgroundDecoration: BoxDecoration(
 2422:           color: reader.readerBackgroundColor,
 2423:         ),
 2424:         childSize: Size(width, height),
 2425:         minScale: 1.0,
 2426:         maxScale: 2.5,
 2427:         strictScale: true,
 2428:         controller: photoViewController,
 2429:         onScaleUpdate: onScaleUpdate,
 2430:         child: SizedBox(width: width, height: height, child: widget),
 2431:       ),
 2432:     );
 2433:   }
 2434: 
 2435:   Widget buildBackground(BuildContext context) {
 2436:     return Column(
 2437:       children: [
 2438:         SizedBox(height: context.padding.top + 16),
 2439:         if (prepareToPrevChapter)
 2440:           _SwipeChangeChapterProgress(
 2441:             controller: scrollController,
 2442:             isPrev: true,
 2443:           ),
 2444:         const Spacer(),
 2445:         if (prepareToNextChapter)
 2446:           _SwipeChangeChapterProgress(
 2447:             controller: scrollController,
 2448:             isPrev: false,
 2449:           ),
 2450:         SizedBox(height: 36),
 2451:       ],
 2452:     );
 2453:   }
 2454: 
 2455:   /// Resolves the scroll-offset delta needed to bring (chapter,page)'s leading
 2456:   /// edge to the viewport's leading edge, or null if that item isn't currently
 2457:   /// laid out. The returned value is added to the current pixels.
 2458:   double? _offsetDeltaToEntry(int chapter, int page) {
 2459:     if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return null;
 2460:     final key = _itemKeys['$chapter:$page'];
 2461:     final ctx = key?.currentContext;
 2462:     final selfBox = context.findRenderObject();
 2463:     if (ctx == null || selfBox is! RenderBox) return null;
 2464:     final itemBox = ctx.findRenderObject();
 2465:     if (itemBox is! RenderBox || !itemBox.attached) return null;
 2466:     final vertical = reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom;
 2467:     final viewportLeading = selfBox.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 2468:     final itemLeading = itemBox.localToGlobal(Offset.zero);
 2469:     return vertical
 2470:         ? itemLeading.dy - viewportLeading.dy
 2471:         : _reverse
 2472:             ? viewportLeading.dx + selfBox.size.width -
 2473:                 (itemLeading.dx + itemBox.size.width)
 2474:             : itemLeading.dx - viewportLeading.dx;
 2475:   }
 2476: 
 2477:   /// Scrolls (animated or instant) so that (chapter,page) sits at the leading
 2478:   /// edge.
 2479:   ///
 2480:   /// Explicit jumps (no [maxStepExtent]) re-pivot the scroll view onto the
 2481:   /// target instead of scrolling to it — see [_repivot]. Scrolling can only be
 2482:   /// exact for the pivot itself: the extent of every entry between the pivot
 2483:   /// and the target is baked into the target's offset, and until an image has
 2484:   /// loaded its entry is a fixed placeholder, so those offsets are wrong by
 2485:   /// pages and keep shifting as the images arrive.
 2486:   ///
 2487:   /// Tap-to-turn keeps the scrolling path: its target is the adjacent entry,
 2488:   /// laid out or at most a couple of viewports away, and rapid taps are meant
 2489:   /// to advance gradually rather than teleport. For an off-screen target we
 2490:   /// iterate: estimate a scroll offset from the index distance to a laid-out
 2491:   /// reference item, jump, let the frame lay out, then read the real delta
 2492:   /// and correct.
 2493:   Future<void> _goToEntry(
 2494:     int chapter,
 2495:     int page, {
 2496:     required bool animate,
 2497:     bool center = false,
 2498:     bool Function()? isCurrent,
 2499:     double? maxStepExtent,
 2500:     Duration? duration,
 2501:   }) async {
 2502:     bool isStale() => isCurrent != null && !isCurrent();
 2503:     void trace(String stage, [Map<String, Object?> extra = const {}]) {
 2504:       final report = debugReaderContinuousNavigation;
 2505:       if (report == null) {
 2506:         return;
 2507:       }
 2508:       final pos = _scrollController.hasClients ? _scrollController.position : null;
 2509:       final intended = _pageTurnCoordinator.intendedTarget;
 2510:       report({
 2511:         'stage': stage, 'chapter': chapter, 'page': page,
 2512:         'actualChapter': reader.chapter, 'actualPage': reader.page,
 2513:         'intendedChapter': intended?.chapter, 'intendedPage': intended?.page,
 2514:         'targetIndex': _indexOfEntry(chapter, page), 'anchor': _anchorIndex,
 2515:         'pixels': pos?.pixels, 'min': pos?.minScrollExtent,
 2516:         'max': pos?.maxScrollExtent, 'stale': isStale(),
 2517:         'delta': _offsetDeltaToEntry(chapter, page),
 2518:         'images': [for (final (entry, box) in _laidOutImages()) {
 2519:           'chapter': entry.chapter, 'page': entry.page,
 2520:           'width': box.size.width, 'height': box.size.height,
 2521:           'delta': _offsetDeltaToEntry(entry.chapter, entry.page),
 2522:         }], ...extra,
 2523:       });
 2524:     }
 2525:     trace('start');
 2526:     if (debugReaderContinuousNavigation != null) {
 2527:       WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
 2528:         if (mounted) {
 2529:           trace('nextFrame');
 2530:         }
 2531:       });
 2532:     }
 2533:     if (isStale()) return;
 2534: 
 2535:     if (maxStepExtent == null) {
 2536:       if (_indexOfEntry(chapter, page) != _anchorIndex) {
 2537:         _repivot(chapter, page);
 2538:         return;
 2539:       }
 2540:       // The pivot is the one entry whose offset is exact by construction.
 2541:       await _applyScroll(
 2542:         0 - _centerAdjust(chapter, page, center),
 2543:         animate: animate,
 2544:         duration: duration,
 2545:       );
 2546:       return;
 2547:     }
 2548: 
 2549:     if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;
 2550: 
 2551:     // A precise offset is only valid for the measured frame. Rebuilding an
 2552:     // earlier footer/image can change its extent in the next sliver layout.
 2553:     // Keep the coordinator's target until actual geometry has stabilized.
 2554:     Future<void> alignLaidOutTarget() async {
 2555:       var stableFrames = 0;
 2556:       for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 8; attempt++) {
 2557:         if (!mounted || !_scrollController.hasClients || isStale()) {
 2558:           return;
 2559:         }
 2560:         final measured = _offsetDeltaToEntry(chapter, page);
 2561:         if (measured == null) {
 2562:           return;
 2563:         }
 2564:         final correction = measured - _centerAdjust(chapter, page, center);
 2565:         if (correction.abs() > 0.5) {
 2566:           stableFrames = 0;
 2567:           await _applyScroll(_scrollController.position.pixels + correction,
 2568:               animate: attempt == 0 && animate, duration: duration);
 2569:         } else {
 2570:           stableFrames++;
 2571:         }
 2572:         await WidgetsBinding.instance.endOfFrame;
 2573:         if (stableFrames >= 2) {
 2574:           return;
 2575:         }
 2576:       }
 2577:     }
 2578: 
 2579:     // Fast path: target already laid out, then verify the next layout.
 2580:     final delta = _offsetDeltaToEntry(chapter, page);
 2581:     if (delta != null) {
 2582:       trace('precise');
 2583:       await alignLaidOutTarget();
 2584:       return;
 2585:     }
 2586: 
 2587:     // Iterative approach for off-screen targets.
 2588:     final targetIdx = _indexOfEntry(chapter, page);
 2589:     final currentIdx = _indexOfEntry(reader.chapter, reader.page);
 2590:     final maxAttempts = math.min(
 2591:       32,
 2592:       math.max(6, (targetIdx - currentIdx).abs() * 2),
 2593:     );
 2594:     for (var attempt = 0; attempt < maxAttempts; attempt++) {
 2595:       if (!_scrollController.hasClients || !mounted || isStale()) return;
 2596:       final pos = _scrollController.position;
 2597: 
 2598:       // Find any currently laid-out image entry to use as a reference point.
 2599:       final ref = _firstLaidOutEntry();
 2600:       if (ref == null) {
 2601:         trace('noReference', {'attempt': attempt});
 2602:         // Nothing measurable yet: step toward the target so an unresolved
 2603:         // middle target cannot be mistaken for the scroll tail.
 2604:         await _applyScroll(
 2605:           pos.pixels + (targetIdx < currentIdx ? -maxStepExtent : maxStepExtent),
 2606:           animate: false,
 2607:         );
 2608:         await WidgetsBinding.instance.endOfFrame;
 2609:         if (isStale()) return;
 2610:         continue;
 2611:       }
 2612: 
 2613:       final refDelta = _offsetDeltaToEntry(ref.chapter, ref.page) ?? 0;
 2614:       final refIdx = _indexOfEntry(ref.chapter, ref.page);
 2615:       if (refIdx == targetIdx) {
 2616:         await alignLaidOutTarget();
 2617:         return;
 2618:       }
 2619:       // Estimate per-entry extent from the reference item's own size.
 2620:       final unit = _entryExtent(ref) ?? (reader.size.height);
 2621:       final estimate = estimateContinuousTurnOffset(
 2622:         currentPixels: pos.pixels,
 2623:         referenceDelta: refDelta,
 2624:         targetIndex: targetIdx,
 2625:         referenceIndex: refIdx,
 2626:         itemExtent: unit,
 2627:         minScrollExtent: pos.minScrollExtent,
 2628:         maxScrollExtent: pos.maxScrollExtent,
 2629:         maxStepExtent: maxStepExtent,
 2630:       );
 2631:       trace('estimate', {
 2632:         'attempt': attempt, 'referenceChapter': ref.chapter,
 2633:         'referencePage': ref.page, 'referenceIndex': refIdx,
 2634:         'referenceDelta': refDelta, 'unit': unit, 'estimate': estimate,
 2635:       });
 2636:       await _applyScroll(estimate, animate: false);
 2637:       await WidgetsBinding.instance.endOfFrame;
 2638:       if (isStale()) return;
 2639: 
 2640:       // Did the target come into layout? If so, finish precisely.
 2641:       final d = _offsetDeltaToEntry(chapter, page);
 2642:       if (d != null) {
 2643:         await alignLaidOutTarget();
 2644:         return;
 2645:       }
 2646:     }
 2647:     trace('exhausted', {'attempts': maxAttempts});
 2648:   }
 2649: 
 2650:   /// Makes (chapter, page) the pivot and shows it at the leading edge.
 2651:   ///
 2652:   /// The pivot is laid out at scroll offset 0 and entries before it grow into
 2653:   /// negative offsets, so no image loading anywhere can move it — the same
 2654:   /// guarantee that makes opening the reader on a restored page exact. Both
 2655:   /// slivers are re-keyed so their children are rebuilt for the new index
 2656:   /// mapping; the per-entry GlobalKeys carry the already-decoded images over.
 2657:   void _repivot(int chapter, int page) {
 2658:     if (!mounted || _entries.isEmpty) return;
 2659:     // _indexOfEntry falls back to the chapter's first image; anchor on what
 2660:     // it resolved to, so later _rebuildEntries calls agree with it.
 2661:     final index = _indexOfEntry(chapter, page);
 2662:     final entry = _entries[index];
 2663:     if (entry.isImage) {
 2664:       chapter = entry.chapter;
 2665:       page = entry.page;
 2666:     }
 2667:     _cancelProgrammaticPageTurn();
 2668:     if (prepareToPrevChapter || prepareToNextChapter) {
 2669:       prepareToPrevChapter = false;
 2670:       prepareToNextChapter = false;
 2671:       jumpToPrevChapter = false;
 2672:       jumpToNextChapter = false;
 2673:       context.readerScaffold.setFloatingButton(0);
 2674:     }
 2675:     setState(() {
 2676:       _anchorChapter = chapter;
 2677:       _anchorPage = page;
 2678:       _anchorIndex = index;
 2679:       _pivotGeneration++;
 2680:       _centerKey = GlobalKey();
 2681:     });
 2682:     // The offset may already be 0, in which case jumpTo stays silent and the
 2683:     // scroll listener would never re-read the page under the new layout.
 2684:     _schedulePositionResolve();
 2685:     if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;
 2686:     _repivoting = true;
 2687:     try {
 2688:       _scrollController.jumpTo(0);
 2689:     } finally {
 2690:       _repivoting = false;
 2691:     }
 2692:   }
 2693: 
 2694:   /// When [center] is on (and vertical), the extra scroll-back offset that
 2695:   /// moves the target page from the viewport's leading edge to vertically
 2696:   /// centered. Only when the page is shorter than the viewport; taller pages
 2697:   /// stay pinned to the top so their top isn't clipped (issue #117-2).
 2698:   double _centerAdjust(int chapter, int page, bool center) {
 2699:     if (!center || reader.mode != ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom) {
 2700:       return 0;
 2701:     }
 2702:     final ctx = _itemKeys['$chapter:$page']?.currentContext;
 2703:     final box = ctx?.findRenderObject();
 2704:     if (box is! RenderBox || !box.attached) return 0;
 2705:     final pageExtent = box.size.height;
 2706:     final viewExtent = reader.size.height;
 2707:     if (pageExtent >= viewExtent) return 0;
 2708:     // Positive value: subtracted from the leading-edge target so equal margins
 2709:     // sit above and below the page.
 2710:     return (viewExtent - pageExtent) / 2;
 2711:   }
 2712: 
 2713:   /// Continuous-mode page turn: move to the adjacent *image* entry in the flat
 2714:   /// [_entries] list, skipping chapter-join separators and crossing chapter
 2715:   /// boundaries (seamless). Scrolling — never a page-number rebuild — so it
 2716:   /// neither reloads the chapter (issue #117-4) nor stalls on a join page
 2717:   /// (issue #117-5). Returns false at a hard boundary (no adjacent image) so
 2718:   /// the caller falls back to chapter navigation.
 2719:   @override
 2720:   bool turnPage(bool forward) {
 2721:     if (_entries.isEmpty) return false;
 2722:     final intended = _pageTurnCoordinator.intendedTarget;
 2723:     final curIdx = intended == null
 2724:         ? _indexOfEntry(reader.chapter, reader.page)
 2725:         : _indexOfEntry(intended.chapter, intended.page);
 2726:     final step = forward ? 1 : -1;
 2727:     final currentEntry = intended ?? _entries[curIdx];
 2728:     final chapterImages = _continuousChapterImages[currentEntry.chapter] ?? reader.images;
 2729:     final atLastImage = chapterImages != null &&
 2730:         currentEntry.page >= chapterImages.length;
 2731:     if (forward &&
 2732:         atLastImage &&
 2733:         _footerPresent(currentEntry.chapter) &&
 2734:         !_footerReached(currentEntry.chapter)) {
 2735:       // Bring the rest of this chapter's comments footer into view. Only a turn
 2736:       // that can actually scroll is consumed; a clamped pending/error footer
 2737:       // must report a hard boundary instead of swallowing every turn forever.
 2738:       final pos = scrollController.hasClients ? scrollController.position : null;
 2739:       final canScroll = pos != null &&
 2740:           pos.hasContentDimensions &&
 2741:           pos.pixels < pos.maxScrollExtent - 1;
 2742:       if (canScroll) {
 2743:         smoothTo(_axis == Axis.vertical ? reader.size.height : reader.size.width);
 2744:         return true;
 2745:       }
 2746:     }
 2747:     for (var i = curIdx + step; i >= 0 && i < _entries.length; i += step) {
 2748:       final entry = _entries[i];
 2749:       if (!entry.isImage) continue;
 2750:       // While this chapter's comments are unresolved the trailing sliver is
 2751:       // clamped at the chapter boundary, so a later chapter's entry cannot be
 2752:       // laid out. Hand the turn to chapter navigation (repivot/remount) instead
 2753:       // of requesting a target this list will never build.
 2754:       if (entry.chapter != reader.chapter &&
 2755:           !reader.chapterCommentsSettled(reader.chapter)) {
 2756:         return false;
 2757:       }
 2758:       if (_boundaryTurnChapter != null) {
 2759:         _turnInteractionGeneration++;
 2760:         _boundaryTurnChapter = null;
 2761:       }
 2762:       _futurePosition = null;
 2763:       unawaited(_pageTurnCoordinator.request(entry));
 2764:       return true;
 2765:     }
 2766:     if (seamlessChapterReading) {
 2767:       final current = intended ?? _entries[curIdx];
 2768:       final chapterImages = _continuousChapterImages[current.chapter];
 2769:       final atBoundary =
 2770:           current.isImage &&
 2771:           chapterImages != null &&
 2772:           (forward ? current.page >= chapterImages.length : current.page <= 1);
 2773:       final adjacentChapter = reader.visibleChapterFrom(
 2774:         current.chapter,
 2775:         forward ? 1 : -1,
 2776:       );
 2777:       if (atBoundary && adjacentChapter != null) {
 2778:         // Same clamp as above: a boundary turn cannot lay the target out until
 2779:         // the footer resolves, so fall back to chapter navigation.
 2780:         if (!reader.chapterCommentsSettled(current.chapter)) return false;
 2781:         _requestBoundaryTurn(adjacentChapter, forward: forward);
 2782:         return true;
 2783:       }
 2784:     }
 2785:     // No adjacent image in the loaded window — let the caller try chapter nav.
 2786:     return false;
 2787:   }
 2788: 
 2789:   Future<void> _prepareTurnTarget(
 2790:     _ContinuousReaderEntry entry,
 2791:     bool Function() isCurrent,
 2792:   ) async {
 2793:     if (!mounted || !entry.isImage) return;
 2794:     debugReaderContinuousNavigation?.call({
 2795:       'stage': 'prepareStart', 'chapter': entry.chapter, 'page': entry.page,
 2796:       'actualChapter': reader.chapter, 'actualPage': reader.page,
 2797:     });
 2798:     try {
 2799:       await _precacheImageEntry(entry, context);
 2800:     } catch (_) {
 2801:       // Keep the normal image error UI reachable when warm-up fails.
 2802:     } finally {
 2803:       if (mounted) {
 2804:         debugReaderContinuousNavigation?.call({
 2805:           'stage': 'prepareEnd', 'chapter': entry.chapter, 'page': entry.page,
 2806:           'actualChapter': reader.chapter, 'actualPage': reader.page,
 2807:           'stale': !isCurrent(),
 2808:         });
 2809:       }
 2810:     }
 2811:   }
 2812: 
 2813:   Future<void> _navigateTurnTarget(
 2814:     _ContinuousReaderEntry entry,
 2815:     bool Function() isCurrent,
 2816:   ) {
 2817:     if (!mounted || !entry.isImage || !isCurrent()) return Future.value();
 2818:     final center =
 2819:         appdata.settings.getReaderSetting(
 2820:           reader.cid,
 2821:           reader.type.sourceKey,
 2822:           'readerCenterPageOnTurn',
 2823:         ) ==
 2824:         true;
 2825:     final animate = reader.enablePageAnimation(reader.cid, reader.type);
 2826:     _futurePosition = null;
 2827:     return _goToEntry(
 2828:       entry.chapter,
 2829:       entry.page,
 2830:       animate: animate,
 2831:       center: center,
 2832:       isCurrent: isCurrent,
 2833:       maxStepExtent:
 2834:           (reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom
 2835:               ? reader.size.height
 2836:               : reader.size.width) *
 2837:           2,
 2838:     );
 2839:   }
 2840: 
 2841:   void _requestBoundaryTurn(int chapter, {required bool forward}) {
 2842:     if (_boundaryTurnChapter != null) return;
 2843:     _boundaryTurnChapter = chapter;
 2844:     final generation = _turnInteractionGeneration;
 2845:     unawaited(
 2846:       _ensureContinuousChapterLoaded(chapter)
 2847:           .then((_) {
 2848:             if (!mounted ||
 2849:                 generation != _turnInteractionGeneration ||
 2850:                 _boundaryTurnChapter != chapter) {
 2851:               return;
 2852:             }
 2853:             final images = _continuousChapterImages[chapter];
 2854:             if (images == null || images.isEmpty) return;
 2855:             final targetPage = forward ? 1 : images.length;
 2856:             final range = _chapterEntryRanges[chapter];
 2857:             if (range == null || targetPage > range.length) return;
 2858:             final target = _entries[_indexOfEntry(chapter, targetPage)];
 2859:             unawaited(_pageTurnCoordinator.request(target));
 2860:           })
 2861:           .whenComplete(() {
 2862:             if (_boundaryTurnChapter == chapter) {
 2863:               _boundaryTurnChapter = null;
 2864:             }
 2865:           }),
 2866:     );
 2867:   }
 2868: 
 2869:   @override
 2870:   bool jumpToChapter(int chapter, {bool toLastPage = false}) {
 2871:     // Non-seamless is keyed by chapter and remounts via the caller's rebuild.
 2872:     if (!seamlessChapterReading) return false;
 2873:     // Scrollable only if the chapter is already loaded; else fall back to a
 2874:     // remount+reload (return false) since scrolling can't reach an unloaded one.
 2875:     final images = _continuousChapterImages[chapter];
 2876:     if (images == null || images.isEmpty) return false;
 2877:     final targetPage = toLastPage ? images.length : 1;
 2878:     reader.chapter = chapter;
 2879:     reader.page = targetPage;
 2880:     context.readerScaffold.update();
 2881:     final animate = reader.enablePageAnimation(reader.cid, reader.type);
 2882:     _futurePosition = null;
 2883:     _goToEntry(chapter, targetPage, animate: animate);
 2884:     return true;
 2885:   }
 2886: 
 2887:   /// Scroll-axis extent of a laid-out entry, or null if not laid out.
 2888:   double? _entryExtent(_ContinuousReaderEntry entry) {
 2889:     final ctx = _itemKeys['${entry.chapter}:${entry.page}']?.currentContext;
 2890:     final box = ctx?.findRenderObject();
 2891:     if (box is! RenderBox || !box.attached) return null;
 2892:     return reader.mode == ReaderMode.continuousTopToBottom
 2893:         ? box.size.height
 2894:         : box.size.width;
 2895:   }
 2896: 
 2897:   /// The first image entry that currently has an attached render box.
 2898:   _ContinuousReaderEntry? _firstLaidOutEntry() {
 2899:     for (final (entry, _) in _laidOutImages()) {
 2900:       return entry;
 2901:     }
 2902:     return null;
 2903:   }
 2904: 
 2905:   /// Trailing laid-out image in logical reading order, including RTL.
 2906:   _ContinuousReaderEntry? _lastLaidOutImageEntry() {
 2907:     for (final (entry, _) in _laidOutImages(reverse: true)) {
 2908:       return entry;
 2909:     }
 2910:     return null;
 2911:   }
 2912: 
 2913:   Future<void> _applyScroll(double offset,
 2914:       {required bool animate, Duration? duration}) async {
 2915:     if (!_scrollController.hasClients) return;
 2916:     final pos = _scrollController.position;
 2917:     final target = offset.clamp(pos.minScrollExtent, pos.maxScrollExtent);
 2918:     if (animate) {
 2919:       await _scrollController.animateTo(
 2920:         target,

 2961:   void _onAutoScrollTick(Duration elapsed) {
 2962:     if (_lastAutoTick == null) {
 2963:       _lastAutoTick = elapsed;
 2964:       return;
 2965:     }
 2966:     final dt = (elapsed - _lastAutoTick!).inMicroseconds / 1000000.0;

 2971:     if (next >= pos.maxScrollExtent - 0.5) {
 2972:       // Land on the actual tail before resolving it. A pending/error footer
 2973:       // may fit in one viewport; its current geometry alone is not completion.
 2974:       _scrollController.jumpTo(pos.maxScrollExtent);

 2977:         reader.stopAutoReading();
 2978:       }
 2979:     } else {
 2980:       _scrollController.jumpTo(next);
 2981:     }

   10: /// Applies enhancement once per successful image/parameter signature.
   11: /// In-flight requests are singleflight per State and globally bounded; newer
   12: /// requests replace pending ones. RawImage fit/alignment/zoom stay unchanged.
   13: /// The original remains drawable whenever enhancement is pending/unavailable.
   14: =======
   15: /// Displays a [ui.Image] with the render-time enhancement shader applied.
   16: ///
   17: /// The shader is run exactly once per (image, strength) into an offscreen
   18: /// [ui.Image] which is then handed to [builder] to paint as usual (a
   19: /// [RawImage], or several sharing it). This keeps all of the reader's existing
   20: /// fit / alignment / zoom (PhotoView) behaviour intact and correct, while
   21: /// costing nothing per frame after the one-time GPU pass.

   23: /// If the shader is unavailable or disabled, the original image is drawn

  396:   Map<String, List<String>> _sourceTagGroups() {
  397:     final data = ComicSource.find(sourceKey)?.categoryData;
  398:     if (data == null) return const {};
  399:     final groups = <String, List<String>>{};
  400:     for (final part in data.categories) {
  401:       if (part is! FixedCategoryPart) continue;
  402:       final labels = <String>[];
  403:       for (final item in part.categories) {
  404:         final label = item.label.trim();
  405:         final param = item.target.attributes?['param']?.toString();
  406:         if (label.isNotEmpty && param != null && param != 'rank') {
  407:           labels.add(label);
  408:         }
  409:       }
  410:       if (labels.isNotEmpty) groups[part.title] = labels.toSet().toList();
  411:     }
  412:     return groups;
  413:   }
  414: 
  415:   bool _canMultiSelectGroup(String group) {
  416:     final source = ComicSource.find(sourceKey);
  417:     final params = <String?>[];
  418:     for (final part in source?.categoryData?.categories ?? const <BaseCategoryPart>[]) {
  419:       if (part.title != group) continue;
  420:       for (final item in part.categories) {
  421:         params.add(item.target.attributes?['param']?.toString());
  422:       }
  423:     }
  424:     return filterGroupAllowsMultiple(
  425:       hasServerLoader: _searchData?.loadWithFilters != null,
  426:       group: group,
  427:       params: params,
  428:       declaredGroups: source?.categoryComicsData?.multiSelectGroups,
  429:     );
  430:   }
  431: 
  432:   String? _tagGroupFor(String label) {
  433:     final data = ComicSource.find(sourceKey)?.categoryData;
  434:     if (data == null) return null;
  435:     for (final part in data.categories) {
  436:       if (part is! FixedCategoryPart) continue;
  437:       for (final item in part.categories) {
  438:         if (item.label.trim() == label.trim()) return part.title;
  439:       }
  440:     }
  441:     return null;
  442:   }
  443: 
  444:   List<CategoryFilterSelection> _selectedSearchFilters() {
  445:     final data = ComicSource.find(sourceKey)?.categoryData;
  446:     if (data == null) return const [];
  447:     final selected = <CategoryFilterSelection>[];
  448:     for (final part in data.categories) {
  449:       if (part is! FixedCategoryPart) continue;
  450:       for (final item in part.categories) {
  451:         final label = item.label.trim();
  452:         if (!_tagFilter.contains(label)) continue;
  453:         final attr = item.target.attributes;
  454:         final param = attr?['param']?.toString();
  455:         if (param == null || param == 'rank') continue;
  456:         selected.add(CategoryFilterSelection(
  457:           group: part.title,
  458:           label: label,
  459:           param: param,
  460:           category: attr?['category']?.toString() ?? part.title,
  461:         ));
  462:       }
  463:     }
  464:     return normalizeCategorySelections(selected);
  465:   }

  556: /// Add/edit dialog for a source language; probes the download size on save

  567: class _SourceLanguageEditorState extends State<_SourceLanguageEditor> {
  568:   late final _name = TextEditingController(text: widget.existing?.name ?? '');
  569:   late final _code = TextEditingController(text: widget.existing?.code ?? '');
  570:   late final _model = TextEditingController(
  571:     text: widget.existing?.modelUrl ?? '',
  572:   );
  573:   late final _dict = TextEditingController(
  574:     text: widget.existing?.dictUrl ?? '',
  575:   );
  576:   late var _height = widget.existing?.inputHeight ?? 48;
  577:   var _checking = false;
  578: 
  579:   @override
  580:   void dispose() {
  581:     _name.dispose();
  582:     _code.dispose();
  583:     _model.dispose();
  584:     _dict.dispose();
  585:     super.dispose();
  586:   }
  587: 
  588:   void _applyPreset(String key) {
  589:     setState(() {
  590:       _model.text =
  591:           '$_rapidOcrBase/onnx/PP-OCRv5/rec/${key}_PP-OCRv5_rec_mobile.onnx';
  592:       _dict.text =
  593:           '$_rapidOcrBase/paddle/PP-OCRv5/rec/${key}_PP-OCRv5_rec_mobile/ppocrv5_${key}_dict.txt';
  594:       _height = 48;
  595:     });
  596:   }
  597: 
  598:   Future<void> _submit() async {
  599:     var name = _name.text.trim();
  600:     if (name.isEmpty) {
  601:       context.showMessage(message: "Enter a language name".tl);
  602:       return;
  603:     }
  604:     var code = widget.existing?.code;
  605:     if (code == null) {
  606:       code = TranslationTargets.normalizeCode(_code.text);
  607:       if (code == null) {
  608:         context.showMessage(message: "Enter a valid language code".tl);
  609:         return;
  610:       }
  611:       if (TranslationSources.isTaken(code)) {
  612:         context.showMessage(message: "This language is already in the list".tl);
  613:         return;
  614:       }
  615:     }
  616:     var model = _model.text.trim();
  617:     var dict = _dict.text.trim();
  618:     if (!TranslationSources.isValidUrl(model) ||
  619:         !TranslationSources.isValidUrl(dict)) {
  620:       context.showMessage(message: "Enter http(s) links for both files".tl);
  621:       return;
  622:     }
  623:     setState(() => _checking = true);
  624:     var store = TranslationModelStore.instance;
  625:     var sizes = await Future.wait([
  626:       store.probeSize(model),
  627:       store.probeSize(dict),
  628:     ]);
  629:     if (!mounted) return;
  630:     var language = CustomSourceLanguage(
  631:       code: code,
  632:       name: name,
  633:       modelUrl: model,
  634:       dictUrl: dict,
  635:       inputHeight: _height,
  636:       sizeBytes: sizes.contains(null) ? 0 : sizes[0]! + sizes[1]!,
  637:     );
  638:     TranslationSources.save(language);
  639:     context.pop(language);
  640:   }
  641:
